### [1] [Bayesian Optimization for Learning Nonlinear MPC in Autonomous Agent Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14763)
*Lorenzo Ortolani,Gabriel Voss,Gabriele Beltrami,Francesco Dorati,Tommaso Felice Banfi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需地图的实时自主导航框架，结合滚动时域规划与非线性MPC，利用LiDAR高斯占据表示、A*轨迹搜索、sigmoid障碍屏障函数，并通过TPE贝叶斯优化和GP代理分析参数敏感性，在Unitree Go2四足机器人上实现90%导航成功率及38.9%指标提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态未知环境中，移动机器人实时自主导航仍面临根本性挑战，现有方法依赖地图构建且参数敏感性强，需要一种无需地图、鲁棒且可迁移的导航框架。

Method: 1) LiDAR高斯占据表示构建环境；2) A*搜索生成无碰撞轨迹；3) CasADi/IPOPT非线性MPC跟踪轨迹，内含sigmoid障碍屏障函数；4) 基于TPE的离线贝叶斯优化寻找近优控制器参数；5) 高斯过程代理分析参数敏感性和优化景观。

Result: 在Unitree Go2四足机器人上，仿真参数可直接迁移至硬件，部署时导航成功率达90.0%，仿真环境下评估指标平均提升38.9%。

Conclusion: 所提框架是机器人无关的，仿真中调优的参数可有效迁移至物理硬件且无需额外调参，验证了方法的实用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Real-time autonomous navigation in dynamic, unknown environments remains a fundamental challenge for mobile robotics. We propose a map-free framework that tightly integrates reactive rolling-horizon planning with nonlinear Model Predictive Control (MPC). At each control cycle, a LiDAR-based Gaussian occupancy representation is constructed and used to generate collision-free trajectories via A* search, which are then tracked by a CasADi/IPOPT MPC formulation incorporating a smooth sigmoid obstacle barrier. To improve robustness to parameter sensitivity, we adopt an offline Bayesian optimization scheme based on Tree-structured Parzen Estimators (TPE), which identifies near-optimal controller parameters with respect to a composite navigation objective. In addition, a Gaussian Process surrogate is used to analyze parameter sensitivity and provide insight into the optimization landscape.
  The proposed framework is robot-agnostic and is evaluated on the Unitree Go2 quadruped in simulation using Gazebo, followed by deployment on the physical robot. Experimental results show that parameters tuned in simulation transfer effectively to hardware, maintaining comparable performance without additional tuning. The full system achieves up to a 90.0\% navigation success rate when deployed, along with a 38.9\% average improvement in the evaluation metrics across simulated environments.

</details>


### [2] [Synthetic-to-Real Pipeline for Safe Landing Zone Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14767)
*Shrikant Banerjee,Reza Faieghi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套面向无人机自主着陆的感知与数据生成管线，通过过程化合成数据引擎生成逼真城市环境并自动标注，基于OneFormer架构在纯合成数据上微调进行语义分割，结合欧几里得距离变换(EDT)和动态推理逻辑确定最大内接安全着陆区域。定量基准测试和定性真实场景验证表明，该方法无需人工标注即可实现鲁棒的边缘端自主着陆感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机向高自主化发展时，需要在非合作、非结构化环境中完成无辅助回收，但自主安全着陆需要高保真语义分辨率以区分可通行地形与危险障碍，而标注航拍数据集的稀缺严重阻碍了相关技术开发。

Method: 1) 提出过程化合成数据引擎，通过域随机化生成具有自动语义标注的逼真城市环境；2) 基于Transformer的OneFormer架构在纯合成数据上微调，利用多头自注意力机制获取全局上下文；3) 确定性着陆模块结合欧几里得距离变换(EDT)和动态推理逻辑，识别最大内接安全着陆区域并保持严格的安全缓冲区。

Result: 1) 在UAVid数据集上的定量基准测试证明了鲁棒的语义分割性能；2) 在真实世界无人机视频上的定性验证确认了系统在未见环境中识别无碰撞着陆点的能力。

Conclusion: 高保真过程化仿真能够消除对人工标注的需求，同时为自主无人机回收提供鲁棒的、可边缘部署的态势感知能力，有效弥合了仿真到现实的鸿沟。

Abstract: As Uncrewed Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) transition toward higher levels of autonomy, the ability to perform unassisted recovery in non-cooperative, unstructured environments becomes critical. Achieving safe autonomous landing requires high-fidelity semantic resolution to distinguish navigable terrain from hazardous obstacles, yet development is often hindered by the scarcity of annotated aerial datasets. This work proposes a comprehensive perception and data generation pipeline designed to bridge the sim-to-real gap for autonomous landing tasks. We introduce a procedural synthetic data engine that generates photorealistic urban environments with automated semantic annotations through domain randomization. A Transformer-based OneFormer architecture is fine-tuned exclusively on this synthetic data, leveraging multi-head self-attention mechanisms for global context resolution. To ensure operational safety, a deterministic landing module utilizes a Euclidean Distance Transform (EDT) and dynamic inference logic to identify the largest inscribed safe landing zones while maintaining strict clearance buffers around obstacles. Quantitative benchmarking against the UAVid dataset demonstrates robust semantic segmentation performance, while qualitative validation on real-world UAV footage confirms the system's ability to identify collision-free landing sites in unseen environments. Our results highlight the potential of high-fidelity procedural simulation to eliminate the need for manual annotation while providing robust, edge-deployable situational awareness for autonomous UAV recovery.

</details>


### [3] [Deep Learning-Based Lunar Crater Terrain Relative Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14776)
*Batu Candan,Simone Servadio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合深度学习陨石坑检测器和扩展卡尔曼滤波的地形相对导航(TRN)算法，用于月球着陆器的精确位置估计。该算法通过单目图像检测陨石坑，与全球数据库匹配后输入EKF进行位姿估计，仿真表明即使初始误差达5公里，也能将导航误差降至数百米。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来月球着陆任务需要高精度位置估计，尤其是在地形特征稀疏的危险环境中，传统导航方法难以满足要求。

Method: 1) 使用面向NASA陨石坑检测挑战赛设计的深度学习检测器，从单目图像中分析陨石坑特征；2) 采用匈牙利分配方法进行陨石坑与全球数据库的匹配，结合基于共识的离群点去除；3) 将测量值输入扩展卡尔曼滤波(EKF)，在月心月固(LCLF)参考系中进行位姿估计，并辅以高度信息约束径向漂移。

Result: 即使航天器初始位置偏差高达5公里，TRN也能从中恢复，将导航误差减小到几百米。同时发现维持陨石坑特征对应需要匹配图像分辨率和场景尺度与检测器训练集分布。

Conclusion: 所提深度学习驱动的TRN算法能有效实现月球着陆中的高精度导航定位，但需注意检测器训练数据与任务场景的分辨率/尺度匹配问题。

Abstract: Accurate position estimation is crucial for the successful implementation of future lunar landings using autonomous vehicles, especially in dangerous environments with sparse terrain features. In this paper, we propose a terrain relative navigation (TRN) algorithm combining our deep-learning crater detector, which was designed specifically for the NASA Crater Detection Challenge problem, and an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF). Our detector analyzes crater features from the monocular images acquired from orbit, and their matches with craters from a global database are identified via a Hungarian assignment approach followed by the consensus-based outliers removal method. The estimated measurements are then used to refine an EKF, where spacecraft pose estimation in the Lunar-Centered Lunar-Fixed (LCLF) frame of reference, augmented with altitude aiding information, constrains radial drift. The simulation results indicate that even if the spacecraft is off from its actual location up to 5 km, TRN could recover from this situation, achieving navigation error reduction to a few hundred meters. It should be noted that in order to maintain crater feature correspondences, it is important to match the image resolution and the scales within the scene to the detector training set distribution.

</details>


### [4] [Computing Smooth Geodesics under Two-Sided Curvature Bounds with Applications to Robotics and Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14794)
*Da Chen,Zhenjiang Li,Jean-Marie Mirebeau,Xuecheng Tai,Jinglin Zhang,Wei Zhang,Laurent D. Cohen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman偏微分方程框架的曲率有界测地线模型，通过对平面曲线曲率施加任意上下界约束，实现强几何控制下的光滑路径追踪，并在机器人路径规划与图像曲线结构跟踪中验证了其有效性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面曲线曲率作为二阶最小路径计算中的关键正则项，与光滑性、刚性和弹性等几何性质密切相关。现有方法缺乏对曲率施加任意上下界约束的能力，难以满足对路径几何形态有严格限制的实际应用需求。

Method: 在HJB偏微分方程框架下建立曲率有界测地线模型，对哈密顿量和HJB PDE设计融入曲率边界的离散化方案，通过数值求解器估计模型数值解。

Result: 在机器人路径规划和图像曲线结构跟踪两个应用中，该模型能够找到满足曲率约束的光滑路径，数值实验表明其作为路径求解工具具有强大的能力和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的曲率有界测地线模型为具有曲率范围约束的最小路径计算提供了有效的理论框架和数值方法，在计算物理与几何问题中具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Curvature of planar curves serves as a key regularization term for computing second-order minimal paths, due to its tight relevance to desirable geometric properties such as smoothness, rigidity, and elasticity. In this paper, we tackle a more challenging problem in computational physics and geometry problem: tracking minimal paths whose curvature is constrained by arbitrary upper and lower bounds. For that purpose, we propose a new curvature-bounded geodesic model, developed under the Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) partial differential equation (PDE) framework. It provides strong geometric control over minimal paths by enforcing curvature range constraints, whose paths are smooth and of bounded curvature limitation. We also present a discretization scheme for the Hamiltonian and the HJB PDE incorporating curvature bounds, allowing efficient solver for estimating numerical solutions to the model. Finally, we illustrate the capability of the proposed curvature-bounded geodesic model in applications of robot path planning and curvilinear structures tracking from images. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed curvature-bounded geodesic model serves as a powerful and robust tool for finding satisfactory paths.

</details>


### [5] [TacStyle: Personalizing Tactile Robot Policies using Structured Behavior Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14862)
*Kevin Robledo,Matías I. Torres Galaz,Kumar Dixhant Rai,Shelly Sara Ulman,Tasmia Tasrin,Heramb Nemlekar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出通过语言推理偏好行为而非直接生成，利用结构化潜在空间组织用户偏好，再借助基础模型解析该空间以选择合适的行为参数，从而实现对机器人行为的精细控制，减少偏好标注需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言条件策略虽能让机器人理解"做什么"，但难以实现"怎么做"的精细控制（如施加多大力度），因为需要大量细粒度偏好标签，且直接条件化难以覆盖连续行为范围。

Method: 1) 学习结构化潜在表示，根据轨迹差异组织用户偏好；2) 给定偏好提示时，使用基础模型（如大语言模型）解析该潜在空间，选择能产生期望行为的潜在值。

Result: 在仿真和真实实验中，该方法相比语言条件策略，能以更少的偏好标签实现更精确的用户偏好适应。

Conclusion: 从结构化潜在空间中选择机器人行为，能够在对用户偏好进行精细适配的同时显著降低对偏好标签的需求。

Abstract: Robotic systems that assist humans should be capable of adapting their behaviors to individual user preferences. For instance, users may want a robot arm to adjust the amount of force it applies while folding their laundry or cleaning furniture. Natural language provides an intuitive way for humans to communicate such preferences. Recent progress in language-conditioned robot policies has shown that robots can successfully use language prompts to determine what task to perform. However, extending the same approach to realize how the task should be performed requires detailed labels describing the preferences or styles of trajectories in the task data. Not only is collecting such annotations challenging, but conditioning directly on these labels may also fail to provide fine-grained control over a continuous range of behaviors. For example, it can be difficult to convey the exact force that a robot must apply through abstract instructions like "apply a bit more pressure than before". Therefore, in this work, we propose using language to reason over preferred behaviors instead of directly generating them. We first learn a structured latent representation that organizes user preferences according to differences in the corresponding trajectories. Then, given a preference prompt, we use a foundation model to interpret this latent space and choose a value that produces the desired behavior. Through both simulation and real-world experiments, we show that selecting robot behaviors from an intuitively structured latent space enables more precise adaptation to user preferences while requiring significantly fewer preference labels than language-conditioned policies.

</details>


### [6] [VANDERER: Map-Free Exploration using Future-Aware and Visual-Curiosity-Guided Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14879)
*Venkata Naren Devarakonda,Raktim Gautam Goswami,Prashanth Krishnamurthy,Farshad Khorrami*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VANDERER是一个利用视觉好奇心模块（VCM）引导预训练扩散策略的探索框架，仅依赖单目图像数据即可实现高效环境探索，在仿真环境中比NoMaD平均多探索13.4%的区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统探索方法依赖占用地图生成，在传感器受限场景（如仅单目相机）中难以准确构建占用地图，因此需要开发不依赖深度信息的视觉探索策略。

Method: 提出VANDERER框架，包含视觉好奇心模块（VCM），通过导航世界模型预测动作结果并用好奇心成本评估，利用该成本引导扩散过程生成最大化探索的动作。

Result: 在多种仿真环境中，VANDERER持续优于基线方法，比NoMaD平均多探索13.4%的面积；发现室外环境中视觉好奇心与几何好奇心之间存在直接相关性。

Conclusion: VANDERER能够有效利用视觉与几何好奇心之间的关系，实现传感器受限智能体的高效探索。

Abstract: Mobile agents require efficient exploration strategies to map unseen environments and autonomously plan tasks. Traditional methods rely on generating occupancy maps and optimizing the sequence in which unexplored regions are visited. However, in sensor-constrained settings, such as those limited to monocular cameras, generating accurate occupancy maps is challenging. To address this, we propose VANDERER, an exploration framework that leverages a Visual Curiosity Module (VCM) to guide pre-trained diffusion policies using only monocular image data. This curiosity module predicts the outcomes of proposed actions via a navigation world model and evaluates them through a curiosity cost. The cost then guides the diffusion process toward generating actions that maximize exploration. Evaluated across diverse simulated environments, VANDERER consistently outperforms established baselines, exploring an average of 13.4% more area than NoMaD. Our results reveal a direct correlation between visual and geometric curiosity in outdoor environments, demonstrating that VANDERER can effectively leverage this relationship for efficient exploration using sensor-constrained agents.

</details>


### [7] [DynaHMRC: Decentralized Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Collaboration for Dynamic Tasks with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14882)
*Wenhao Yu,Yu'ang Xie,Yifan Duan,Jie Peng,Guanting Ye,Ka-Veng Yuen,Yanyong Zhang,Jianmin Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DynaHMRC，一种去中心化的多机器人协作框架，每个机器人作为角色感知的LLM智能体，通过四阶段闭环流程解决集中式LLM调度器的可扩展性差、动态任务适应不足和领域数据稀缺问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集中式LLM调度器在大规模异构多机器人系统中的可扩展性差（单模型上下文瓶颈）、动态任务建模不足、以及领域专用数据稀缺导致通用模型在专家任务中效率低下。

Method: 提出去中心化DynaHMRC框架，每个机器人作为角色感知LLM智能体；设计四阶段闭环协作流程（自描述、任务分配与领导竞标、领导者选举、反思执行）；构建覆盖3类任务、4种动态变化和6种团队配置的基准；通过领域专家数据集微调预训练LLM。

Result: 相比强基线方法，DynaHMRC以更少的动作和通信步骤实现了更高的成功率，并在评估设置中展现出随团队规模增长的良好可扩展性趋势。

Conclusion: 去中心化的角色感知LLM智能体框架可有效缓解集中式调度瓶颈，在动态多机器人协作场景中兼顾效率与可扩展性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) provide robots with richer task understanding and adaptability, making them promising for coordinating heterogeneous multi-robot systems in long-horizon tasks. Despite this potential, several challenges remain underexplored: (1) Centralized LLM schedulers scale poorly as team size and environmental complexity increase. A single model must process excessive contextual information, and long-context approximation may degrade reasoning quality; (2) Existing task formulations insufficiently consider dynamic settings, while robust adaptation to evolving task conditions is essential for real-world deployment; (3) Domain-specific data scarcity limits specialized robotic reasoning, making proprietary general-purpose models inefficient for expert tasks. To address these limitations, we propose DynaHMRC, a decentralized framework in which each robot acts as a role-aware LLM agent. This design mitigates the single-model context bottleneck and supports flexible collaboration across heterogeneous team configurations. DynaHMRC organizes collaboration as a four-stage closed-loop process: self-description, task allocation with leadership bidding, leader election, and reflective execution, supported by executable robot interfaces. We further develop a benchmark covering three task families, four dynamic variations, and six team configurations to systematically study dynamic task modeling. In addition, we conduct an empirical analysis to guide the construction of domain-specific expert datasets and fine-tune pretrained LLMs to improve specialized competence. Experiments show that DynaHMRC achieves higher success rates than strong baselines with fewer action and communication steps, while demonstrating promising scalability trends as team size grows within the evaluated settings.

</details>


### [8] [Multimodal Physiological Assessment of Contact-Rich Physical Human-Robot Interaction Under Varying Environmental Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14969)
*Yanyi Chen,Xi Wang,Min Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过多模态实验（18种温/噪/光组合），发现操作者在接触式人机交互中会通过增加生理努力（如皮电活动）来补偿热不适，从而维持任务表现稳定，但传统任务指标无法捕捉这一隐藏的生理代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统任务导向评估忽视了真实环境中温度、噪声、光照等压力源对操作者造成的生理负担，需要揭示这些隐藏的生理代价。

Method: 开展了多模态实验，包含18种温度、噪声、光照组合下的接触式追踪任务，同步采集EDA、sEMG、眼动数据及主观舒适度评分，并对比生理信号与执行数据。

Result: 任务表现（追踪误差、完成时间）在各条件下保持稳定；自主神经负荷（tonic SCL）随温度升高而增加，而身体和认知负荷不受影响；主观舒适度与任务表现无显著关联。

Conclusion: 操作者通过增加生理努力来补偿热不适以维持表现，揭示了生理-行为补偿机制，启发开发基于实时生理指标的生理感知控制架构以降低操作者负荷。

Abstract: Physical human-robot interaction (pHRI) in real-world settings exposes operators to fluctuating environmental conditions during contact-rich tasks. Traditional task-centric evaluations overlook the physiological burdens imposed by these stressors. Therefore, we conducted a multimodal empirical study involving contact-rich tracing tasks under 18 distinct combinations of temperature, acoustic noise, and illuminance. Synchronously, we recorded electrodermal activity (EDA), surface electromyography (sEMG), eye-tracking data, and subjective environmental comfort ratings. Evaluating these physiological signals alongside execution data revealed hidden physiological costs not captured by objective performance. The results revealed that task performance remained stable across all environmental conditions. Autonomic workload, indexed by tonic skin conductance level (SCL), increased with temperature, while physical and cognitive workload were unaffected. Perceived environmental comfort showed no significant association with tracing error or completion time. These findings reveal a compensatory mechanism where operators maintain consistent performance by increasing their physiological effort to suppress thermal discomfort. Such insight motivates the development of physiology-aware control architectures that leverage real-time physiological metrics to reduce operator workload in unstructured environments.

</details>


### [9] [Inference-time Policy Steering via Vision and Touch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14981)
*Yilin Wu,Zilin Si,Zeynep Temel,Oliver Kroemer,Andrea Bajcsy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ViTaL提出了一个视觉-触觉联合的推理时策略引导框架，通过双层优化实现全局行为选择与局部触觉约束满足，在接触丰富的操控任务中将成功率提升51%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时策略引导方法仅依赖视觉观测，但在接触丰富的操控任务中，视觉信息不足以感知接触力等细微局部交互，导致任务成功率受限。作者旨在融合视觉与触觉信息，在推理阶段同时满足全局任务进展和局部接触约束。

Method: 提出ViTaL框架，将多模态引导形式化为双层优化问题：高层通过视觉采样与验证进行长时域模式选择（决定机器人应执行何种行为）；低层通过触觉引导的扩散编辑在短时域内优化选定动作序列以满足局部接触需求。同时学习视觉-触觉潜在世界模型，并构建语义对齐的视觉与触觉验证器（包括一种新颖的文本条件触觉奖励函数，直接在潜在空间中对预测的触觉未来状态进行评分）。

Result: 在三个真实世界的接触丰富操控任务上，ViTaL相比基础策略提升51%成功率，相比单模态引导提升至少33%，相比朴素多模态融合提升至少20%。

Conclusion: 视觉与触觉的多模态融合在推理时策略引导中具有显著优势，双层优化框架能有效解耦全局行为选择与局部接触约束，为接触丰富的机器人操控提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Inference-time steering adapts pre-trained generative robot policies during deployment by verifying candidate actions before execution. While prior methods typically perform this verification only with visual observations, vision alone is often insufficient for contact-rich manipulation, where success depends on both global task progress and subtle local interactions such as contact force. We introduce ViTaL, a visuo-tactile inference-time steering framework that formulates multimodal guidance as a bi-level optimization problem. At the high level, visual sampling-and-verification performs long-horizon mode selection, deciding what behavior the robot should execute. At the low level, tactile-guided diffusion editing refines the selected action sequence over a shorter horizon to satisfy local contact requirements. To support outcome-based steering, ViTaL learns a visuo-tactile latent world model and employs semantically aligned visual and tactile verifiers, including a novel text-conditioned tactile reward that scores predicted tactile futures directly in latent space. Across three real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, ViTaL improves overall success by 51% over the base policy, outperforms unimodal steering by at least 33%, and exceeds naive multimodal fusion by at least 20%. Website: https://yilin-wu98.github.io/vital_website.

</details>

*Sheng Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LV-Calib提出了一种使用可打印平面靶标进行激光雷达-相机外参标定和激光雷达边界响应校准的框架，通过圆形反射率边界提供结构特征点，并利用迭代优化实现亚像素重投影精度和毫米级特征一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的激光雷达-相机标定方法在处理激光雷达有限波束足迹和黑白反射率不连续处引起的混合强度返回导致的边界展宽和失真问题时精度不足，需要一种能够自动处理这些误差的标定框架。

Method: 1) 使用带有视觉基准和圆形反射率边界的可打印平面靶标；2) 自动裁剪背景点并估计靶标平面；3) 结合强度和几何约束迭代精化3D特征点；4) 构建加权重投影一致性外参优化，融合图像重投影残差和加权的激光雷达特征残差；5) 利用估计的外参和提取的过渡带，通过边界重叠样本的俯仰-偏航-距离残差统计来校准激光雷达边界响应。

Result: 在印刷板标定数据上实现了亚像素重投影精度、毫米级激光雷达特征一致性，并提升了里程计性能。

Conclusion: LV-Calib通过可打印靶标和迭代精化策略，有效解决了激光雷达-相机外参标定中因混合返回导致的边界失真问题，实现了高精度标定，并将开源代码和标定数据以支持可复现评估。

Abstract: We present LV-Calib, a calibration framework for LiDAR-camera extrinsic estimation and LiDAR boundary-response calibration using a printable planar target. The target serves as a shared observation carrier: visual fiducials provide indexed image measurements, while circular reflectivity boundaries provide LiDAR-observable structural feature points. Instead of directly fitting boundary points as ideal geometric contours, LV-Calib automatically crops background points, estimates the target plane, and iteratively refines accurate LiDAR-side 3-D feature points from intensity and geometric constraints. The refinement explicitly handles the broadened and distorted transition band induced by finite beam footprint and mixed-intensity returns around black-white reflectivity discontinuities. Given these refined LiDAR features, we formulate a weighted reprojection-consistent extrinsic optimization with LiDAR feature alignment, where image observations are kept in the reprojection domain and LiDAR feature residuals are weighted by refinement confidence. Finally, using the estimated extrinsic and the extracted transition band, LV-Calib calibrates the LiDAR boundary response by estimating pitch-yaw-range residual statistics of boundary-overlap samples. Experiments on printed-board calibration data demonstrate sub-pixel reprojection accuracy, millimeter-level LiDAR feature consistency, and improved odometry performance. Code and calibration data will be released for reproducible evaluation.

</details>

*Jason Chan,Jonathan C. Kao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Token Steering (TS) 是一种无需额外训练或微调的方法，通过在推理时直接干预视觉-语言-动作模型的动作令牌空间，注入低维用户输入来动态引导机器人轨迹生成，在两个家务操作任务中将成功率从10.0%/16.7%提升至72.5%/93.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）机器人基础模型虽然具备灵活的任务执行能力，但缺乏用户实时引导轨迹生成的手段。现有方法要么需要修改模型架构或重新训练，要么无法在保持预训练策略灵巧性和任务先验的同时进行轻量级干预。

Method: TS 在推理阶段将低维用户输入直接注入到自回归 VLA 模型的原生动作令牌表示空间中，用户输入引导而非覆盖预训练策略，所有操作均在推理时完成，无需训练或微调。

Conclusion: TS 实现了轻量级、直观的机器人基础模型引导，有望改善消费环境中的日常人机交互，并扩大对行动能力受限个体的可及性。

Abstract: We present Token Steering (TS), a method for dynamically steering trajectories generated by an autoregressive vision-language-action (VLA) model through direct intervention in the action-token space. TS injects low-dimensional user inputs into the model's native action-token representation, allowing users to influence trajectory generation without modifying the underlying vision-language model (VLM) architecture. Because TS operates entirely at inference time, it requires no additional training or finetuning. User inputs guide rather than override the pretrained policy, allowing users to influence robot actions while preserving the dexterity, smoothness, and task priors learned by the VLA. We evaluate TS on two household manipulation tasks -- drawer closing after object placement and state-aware object swapping -- and improve success rates from 10.0% to 72.5% and from 16.7% to 93.8%, respectively. By enabling lightweight, intuitive steering over robot foundation models, our interface has the potential to improve human-robot interaction in consumer environments and broaden accessibility for individuals with limited physical control. Project website: https://jasontchan.github.io/token-steering/ .

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发首个亚克级(subgram)全自主微型游泳机器人，突破传统微机器人依赖外部供电或控制的限制，实现机载电源、驱动和计算的集成。

Method: 采用两个10mg SMA线驱动的HWD执行器作为推进装置；设计定制PCB和11mAh 3.7V 507mg单节锂电池实现机载电源与计算；整体重量控制在900mg。

Conclusion: Swima是首个具备机载电源、驱动和计算能力的亚克级微型游泳机器人，展示了微尺度自主机器人的可行性和潜力。

Abstract: We present the Swima, a bioinspired 900-mg swimmer propelled by two 10-mg high-work-density (HWD) actuators driven by shape-memory alloy (SMA) wires. We integrated onboard power and computation by using a custom-built printed circuit board (PCB) and an 11-mAh 3.7-V 507-mg single-cell lithium-ion (Li-Ion) battery, which in conjunction enable autonomous swimming in excess of 18 min. The Swima can swim at speeds of up to 22.4 mm/s (0.56 Bl/s), achieves turning rates of up to 14°/s, and can follow 0-degree heading reference trajectories with root mean square (RMS) values of tracking errors of about 6.5° across multiple tests. This robot is the first subgram microswimmer with onboard power, actuation, and computation developed to date.

### [13] [Exact, Efficient, and Safe Occlusion-Aware Planning Using AH-Polyhedrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15046)
*Long Kiu Chung,David Isele,Toktam Mohammadnejad,Faizan M. Tariq,Sangjae Bae,Shreyas Kousik,Jovin D'sa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出APRO框架，基于博弈论主动感知和AH-多面体可达性分析，实现了自动驾驶代客泊车场景下精确且高效的遮挡感知规划，通过将安全条件重构为AH-多面体并利用线性规划进行精确验证，在不引入额外保守性和道路拓扑假设的前提下实现了100%的安全率与实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶代客泊车场景中遮挡频繁且交通规则宽松，现有方法要么缺乏形式化安全保障，要么假设车辆遵循道路结构，要么过于保守导致车辆被困，遮挡感知规划仍是一个开放挑战。

Method: 将先前工作中的基于集合的安全条件重构为AH-多面体的并集，通过线性规划实现精确的安全性验证，并将安全条件集成到基于优化的规划器或二分搜索方案中以满足实时应用需求。

Result: 在仿真和硬件实验中，包括真实停车场数据集回放测试，APRO在所有评估场景中均实现了100%的安全率，同时保持实时性能，比现有具有形式化安全保障的方法实现了更安全、更优化的决策。

Conclusion: APRO通过AH-多面体可达性分析和博弈论主动感知，在不引入任何额外保守性和道路拓扑假设的前提下，为解决自动驾驶代客泊车中的遮挡感知规划问题提供了一种精确、高效且具有形式化安全保障的解决方案。

Abstract: Safely handling occlusions is a fundamental challenge for autonomous mobile robots operating in dynamic environments. This issue is especially prominent in autonomous valet parking (AVP), where traffic rules are lax, occlusions are frequent and cluttered, and overly conservative behavior can leave vehicles stuck. However, existing methods either lack formal safety guarantees, assume agents follow road structures, or introduce conservatism, leaving occlusion-aware planning for AVP an open challenge. In this paper, we propose APRO (AH-Polyhedron Reachability for Occlusions), an exact and efficient occlusion-aware planning framework based on game-theoretic active perception and AH-polyhedron reachability analysis with AVP as our canonical use case. Our key insight is to reformulate set-based safety conditions in prior work as unions of AH-polyhedrons, enabling exact safety verification through linear programming (LP) without any additional conservatism in set computations or assumptions on road topology. We further show how the resulting safety conditions can be integrated into optimization-based planners or a bisection search scheme for real-time applications. We validate our method in simulation and hardware experiments, including data replay on a real-world parking lot dataset. Experimental results demonstrate that our method consistently achieved a 100% safety rate across all evaluated scenarios while maintaining real-time performance, resulting in safer and more optimal decisions than existing methods with formal safety guarantees.

</details>


### [14] [Design and Fabrication of a Spin Coater with In-Situ Optical Measurement for Soft Thin Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15068)
*Daniel Gliksberg,Jiajie Qiu,Jun Suzuki,Kamal Youcef-Toumi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种低成本的3D打印旋涂机，集成了光学厚度测量系统，用于软弹性体薄膜的精确制备，厚度测量分辨率为3.6-3.7微米，重复性在10微米以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋涂法制备软弹性体薄膜时，接触式测量会导致形变，传统光学测量成本高且复杂；而薄膜厚度对介电弹性体致动器(DEA)等应用的机械和功能性能影响显著，因此需要一种低成本、非接触式的厚度测量与控制系统。

Method: 设计并3D打印了旋涂机主体，集成光学测量系统：通过激光反射至象限光电探测器(QPD)跟踪位移来原位测量厚度，避免形变；讨论了光学几何、传感器线性约束及有限元分析结构验证；使用校准金属垫片和硅胶薄膜进行实验验证。

Result: 厚度分辨率达3.6-3.7微米，最佳重复性为13微米（95%置信区间）；系统可重复制备厚度偏差在9微米以内的硅胶薄膜。

Conclusion: 将低成本光学计量集成到旋涂系统中是可行的，能在无需专用工业设备的情况下实现软薄膜的厚度可控制备。

Abstract: Spin coating is widely used for fabrication of thin polymer and elastomer films, yet reliable thickness verification of highly compliant materials remains challenging due to deformation from contact-based measurements and the cost and complexity of conventional optical metrology. Accurate thickness control is especially critical in soft elastomer applications such as dielectric elastomer actuators (DEAs), where mechanical and functional performance scales strongly with film thickness. This work presents a low-cost, primarily 3D-printed benchtop spin coater with an integrated, minimally deforming optical thickness measurement system for soft-film fabrication workflows. The system is designed to manufacture films between 50 and 300 microns thick with repeatability within 10 microns. Thickness is measured in-situ by tracking displacement of a reflected laser beam via quadrant photodetector, avoiding significant deformation. Optical geometry, sensor linearity constraints, and structural validation via finite element analysis are discussed. Experimental validation using calibrated metal shims demonstrated a thickness resolution of 3.6-3.7 microns and best-case measurement repeatability of 13 microns (95 percent confidence interval). The platform repeatably produced silicone films within 9 microns of target thickness, demonstrating that accessible optical metrology can be integrated into a low-cost spin coating system for practical, thickness-controlled fabrication of compliant thin films without specialized industrial instrumentation.

</details>


### [15] [DragMesh-2: Physically Plausible Dexterous Hand-Object Interaction with Articulated Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15133)
*Tianshan Zhang,Yijia Duan,Yanjun Li,Zeyu Zhang,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DragMesh-2 是一个接触驱动的灵巧交互框架，将关节物体的交互从以物体为中心的生成扩展到手部驱动的灵巧手-物体交互，并通过 PICA 机制在无触觉/力反馈下注入物理信号以提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关节物体操作与静态物体不同：目标部件无法直接驱动，其运动必须通过手-手柄的持续物理接触产生。几何轨迹回放或开环执行无法建模关节部件运动所需的接触动力学，且固定动力学下训练的策略会过度拟合标称接触载荷，在载荷变化时性能下降。

Method: 提出 DragMesh-2 接触驱动框架，将关节交互从以物体为中心的生成扩展到手部驱动的灵巧交互；提出 PICA（物理信息接触感知训练机制），在无触觉或力反馈下将物理信号注入策略学习；在不同阻尼条件和关节物体类别下进行系统评估。

Result: 在七个 GAPartNet 物体上，DragMesh-2 在接触载荷变化下比对比方法具有更强的鲁棒性，同时在不同阻尼条件下保持高任务成功率。

Conclusion: DragMesh-2 实现了从物体为中心的关节运动生成到手驱动灵巧交互的有效过渡，PICA 机制提升了无触觉反馈下的策略鲁棒性，并为未来人形手-物体交互研究提供了纯几何灵巧交互资源。

Abstract: Dexterous interaction with articulated objects is important for household, assistive, and humanoid manipulation, where multi-finger hands can provide compliant contact patterns beyond parallel-jaw grasping. However, articulated-object manipulation differs from static-object manipulation: the target part cannot be directly actuated, and its motion must emerge through sustained physical hand--handle contact. This makes the transition from object-centric articulated generation to hand-driven dexterous hand--object interaction non-trivial, since geometric trajectory replay or open-loop execution does not model the contact dynamics required to move the articulated part. Moreover, policies trained only for task completion under fixed dynamics can overfit nominal contact loads, especially without tactile or force feedback, and may degrade when the contact load changes. To address these challenges, we present DragMesh-2, a contact-driven framework for dexterous interaction with articulated objects that extends articulated interaction from object-centric generation to hand-driven dexterous hand--object interaction, where articulated motion must arise through physical contact. We further propose PICA, a physically informed contact-aware training mechanism that injects physical signals into policy learning without tactile or force feedback, improving robustness and task success under changing contact loads. Finally, we conduct systematic evaluation across multiple damping conditions and articulated-object categories to study robustness under contact-load variation, and provide a pure-geometry dexterous interaction resource to support future loco-manipulation and humanoid hand--object interaction research. Across seven GAPartNet objects, DragMesh-2 achieves stronger robustness under contact-load variation than the compared methods while maintaining high task success across damping conditions.

</details>


### [16] [MimicIK: Real-Time Generative Inverse Kinematics from Teleoperation with FK Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15148)
*Jiahao Yang,Shenhao Yan,Fan Feng,Chengsi Yao,Ge Wang,Zhixin Mai,Yiming Zhao,Yatong Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MimicIK是一个基于条件流匹配的实时生成式逆运动学框架，通过学习遥操作演示中的平滑关节空间运动先验，实现高精度、高平滑度且鲁棒的实时机器人控制，在6-DOF机器人数据集上达到4.65mm位置误差、92.01%成功率，推理延迟仅6.74ms，支持20Hz实时控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值IK求解器在闭环部署中存在不连续分支切换和奇异点附近不稳定问题，而学习的IK方法在空间精度、运动平滑性和实时效率之间难以平衡，尤其是在噪声人类遥操作数据上训练时。

Method: 提出MimicIK框架，基于条件流匹配（Conditional Flow Matching）从遥操作演示中学习平滑鲁棒的关节空间运动先验；采用以最小迭代策略（MIP）为骨干的两步迭代精炼过程预测连续增量关节指令；引入可微的正向运动学一致性损失（FK consistency loss）作为训练正则化项，惩罚任务空间偏离目标位姿的误差。

Result: 在包含8,848条遥操作演示的真实6-DOF机器人数据集上，MimicIK达到4.65mm平均位置误差、92.01%的10mm成功率、仅7.99%轨迹尖峰率；相比UNet扩散基线，推理延迟从21.66ms降至6.74ms；相比确定性MLP基线，在奇异配置附近保持稳定，实现20Hz实时控制。

Conclusion: MimicIK通过生成式条件流匹配有效结合了传统数值IK的几何精度与学习方法的平滑性，在空间精度、运动平滑性和实时效率三个维度上均优于现有方法，为实时机器人操作中的逆运动学问题提供了鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Inverse kinematics (IK) remains a critical bottleneck for real-time robot manipulation. Classical numerical solvers achieve high geometric precision but often suffer from discontinuous branch switching and unstable behavior near kinematic singularities during closed-loop deployment. Meanwhile, learned IK approaches frequently struggle to balance spatial accuracy, motion smoothness, and real-time efficiency, particularly when trained on noisy human teleoperation data. We present \textbf{MimicIK}, a real-time generative inverse kinematics framework that learns smooth and robust joint-space motion priors from teleoperation demonstrations through conditional flow matching. Given the current joint configuration and a target end-effector pose, MimicIK predicts continuous delta-joint commands using an efficient two-step iterative refinement process based on a Minimal Iterative Policy (MIP) backbone. To enforce physical consistency, we further introduce an FK consistency loss, a differentiable forward-kinematics regularization that penalizes task-space deviations from the target pose during training. We evaluate MimicIK on a real-world 6-DOF robot dataset containing 8,848 teleoperation demonstrations. MimicIK achieves a mean position error of 4.65 mm, a 10 mm success rate of 92.01\%, and a trajectory spike rate of only 7.99\%. Compared with a UNet diffusion baseline, our method improves both spatial accuracy and motion smoothness while reducing inference latency from 21.66 ms to 6.74 ms. Furthermore, unlike deterministic MLP baselines that catastrophically diverge under out-of-distribution deployment, MimicIK remains stable near singular configurations and enables robust 20 Hz real-time control on deployment hardware.

</details>


### [17] [Task-Aware Environment Augmentation for Reliable Navigation via Shielded Conditional Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15154)
*Bharawee Phoompho,Gokul Puthumanaillam,Yan Miao,Ruben Hernandez,Tim Bretl,Sayan Mitra,Melkior Ornik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SCoDA方法，通过条件扩散模型学习在已知环境中为规划轨迹布置有限数量的视觉标记物，以增强局部观测能力，从而在部分可观测条件下提升轨迹执行的可靠性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常在固定环境中通过置信空间规划、主动定位等方式适应不确定性，但在观测贫瘠区域表现脆弱且计算成本高昂。作者提出反转思路，通过环境增强（布置标记物）来提升轨迹执行可靠性，解决"任务感知的环境增强"这一开放问题。

Method: SCoDA基于条件扩散模型，以环境地图、规划轨迹、扰动上下文和期望执行剖面为条件，学习高质量标记物布局的分布。其屏蔽采样器推理轨迹上需要位姿校正的位置，引导分布生成符合任务需求且满足预算约束的增强方案。

Result: 在仿真基准和真实硬件部署中，SCoDA相比强基线方法显著提升了轨迹执行可靠性和完成时间。

Conclusion: 少量精心布置的视觉标记物即可有效防止状态估计误差沿任务轨迹累积，SCoDA通过数据驱动的方式学习这种布局策略，为部分可观测下的可靠规划提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reliable trajectory planning under partial observability depends not only on computing a feasible geometric path, but also on whether the robot receives informative observations while executing that trajectory. Existing approaches usually keep the environment fixed and adapt the robot through belief-space planning, active localization, or added sensing, often incurring costly uncertainty propagation and brittle behavior in observation-poor regions. We flip this perspective and address the largely open problem of \emph{task-aware environment augmentation}: given a mapped environment, a planned task trajectory, and a small budget of visual fiducial markers, where should the environment be augmented so that the planned trajectory can be executed reliably under uncertainty? Our key observation is that useful marker layouts are defined by the localization support they provide along the task trajectory: a small number of well-timed observations can be sufficient to prevent uncertainty from accumulating in regions where state-estimation error would otherwise compromise control. Building on this observation, we present \tbp{SCoDA}, $\textbf{S}$hielded $\textbf{Co}$nditional $\textbf{D}$iffusion for Environment $\textbf{A}$ugmentation. \tbp{SCoDA} learns a conditional distribution over high-performing fiducial layouts from data, using the environment, planned trajectory, disturbance context, and desired execution profile as conditioning. Its shielded sampler reasons over where along the planned execution pose corrections should occur, and steers this distribution toward task-relevant, finite-budget augmentations. Across simulated benchmarks and hardware deployments, we show that \tbp{SCoDA} improves trajectory execution reliability and completion time over strong baselines.
  Code, models and dataset available at: \hyperlink{scoda-diffusion.github.io}{https://scoda-diffusion.github.io/}

</details>


### [18] [Seam-to-Graph Reconstruction for Garment Configuration Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15171)
*Xuzhao Huang,Kai Tang,Fuyuki Tokuda,Norman C. Tien,Kazuhiro Kosuge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于图神经网络和注意力机制的Seam-to-Graph网络，将部分可观测的接缝信息映射为结构骨架图用于服装状态估计，并设计变形感知的层次化视觉伺服控制器，在双机器人系统上实现了与人类水平相当的服装配置对齐精度且具有更强的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服装的接缝包含丰富的结构信息，但在机器人操作场景中通常只能被部分观测，需要一种能够鲁棒地利用不完全接缝信息的方法来进行服装状态估计和精确操控。

Method: 提出Seam-to-Graph网络，基于图神经网络和注意力机制将非结构化接缝观测映射为具有拓扑编码的结构骨架图，实现实时服装状态估计；基于骨架图状态估计设计变形感知的层次化视觉伺服控制器，用于服装配置对齐。

Result: 在双机器人系统上将服装装载到丝网印刷台并对齐到期望配置的实验表明，该方法达到了与人类水平相当的对齐精度，且对齐误差方差更小，对不同服装具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 接缝信息在服装操作中非常有效，所提方法能够实现精准且鲁棒的服装配置对齐。

Abstract: Seams encode rich structural information about garments but are frequently partially observable in robotic manipulation scenarios. To robustly leverage seam information, we propose a Seam-to-Graph network based on graph neural networks and attention mechanisms. This network maps unstructured seam observations to a topology-encoded structural skeleton graph for real-time garment state estimation. Using this skeleton-graph-based state estimation, we design a deformation-aware, hierarchical visual servoing controller for garment configuration alignment. We implement this controller on a bimanual robot system to load a garment onto a screen printing platen and to align it to the desired configuration precisely. Real-robot experiments demonstrate that the robot using the proposed method not only achieves human-level alignment accuracy with reduced variance in alignment error but is also robust to different garments. These results demonstrate that the use of seam information is effective for garment manipulation.

</details>


### [19] [Rethinking Implicit Spatial Representation in Visuomotor Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15232)
*Xiangyu Chen,Yuxuan Hu,Chuhao Zhou,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了空间softmax池化在机器人操作模仿学习中的作用，发现其能产生紧凑稳定的空间表征，但受限于视觉编码器中的表征瓶颈（重复下采样削弱了细粒度空间信息）。为此提出PRISM编码器，通过自上而下的交叉注意力融合保留多尺度隐式空间信息，在低分辨率高精度任务ToolHang上将成功率从5.0%提升至13.4%，参数仅增加15.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管空间softmax表征已被广泛应用于先前的视觉运动策略中，但其有效性和底层机制仍缺乏充分理解。本文旨在系统研究不同池化方法，揭示空间softmax池化在机器人操作任务中的优势与局限性。

Method: 通过系统对比不同池化方法在视觉编码器中的表现，发现空间softmax池化产生紧凑稳定的空间表征。针对其表征瓶颈（重复下采样丢失细粒度信息），提出PRISM编码器，采用自上而下的交叉注意力融合机制保留多尺度隐式空间信息。

Result: 在多个任务和策略主干上取得一致性改进。尤其在低分辨率高精度的ToolHang任务中，平均成功率从5.0%提升至13.4%，参数量仅增加15.4%。

Conclusion: 多尺度隐式空间表征是机器人操作中一种有效且高效的设计原则，支持其作为视觉运动策略的通用设计范式。

Abstract: Generative model-based imitation learning has become a widely adopted paradigm for robotic manipulation, where policy performance depends critically on the conditioned visual representations. Although spatial softmax-based representations have been adopted in prior visuomotor policies, their effectiveness and underlying mechanisms remain insufficiently understood. This work rethinks the use of spatial softmax pooling: do such implicit spatial representations provide effective and stable visual features for robotic manipulation? Through systematic studies of different pooling methods in visual encoders, we find that this pooling operation produces compact and stable spatial representations, which outperform feature-value representations, despite using substantially fewer dimensions. Complementary saliency analysis further suggests that these spatial representations guide the encoder to focus more consistently on task-relevant regions. However, this advantage is limited by a representation bottleneck in current visual encoders: repeated downsampling operations weaken fine-grained spatial information before the action-generation module can use it, especially under low-resolution observations. Motivated by these findings, we propose PRISM, a visual encoder that preserves multiscale implicit spatial information through top-down cross-attention fusion. Experiments across multiple tasks and policy backbones show consistent improvements. In particular, on the low-resolution, high-precision ToolHang task, PRISM shows clear gains, improving the average success rate from 5.0% to 13.4% while increasing parameters by only 15.4%. These results support the use of multiscale implicit spatial representations as an effective and efficient design principle for robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [20] [Co-Creating Buildable and Open Social Robot Study Companions with University Students](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15239)
*Farnaz Baksh,Matevž B. Zorec,Feiazie Baksh,Karl Kruusamäe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过应用DfA/DfD设计方法和双钻石框架，对开源社交机器人RSC v4.1的底盘进行机械重设计（采用扭锁紧固件、卡扣接头和无工具维修闩锁），使系统可用性从差（SUS 59.4）提升至优秀（SUS 89.4），感知工作量趋势下降（NASA-TLX 4.29→4.00），平均组装时间从21.4分钟降至13.7分钟，证明了机械重设计能降低开源硬件构建门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源社交机器人虽然具有可获取性、可修复性和学生赋能等优势，但构建过程本身往往成为障碍——现有平台要么预组装好（剥夺动手学习机会），要么让学生面对不熟悉的紧固件、混乱布线和难以触及的维修点，削弱了参与感。需要验证针对性的机械重设计是否能在保持结构完整性的同时降低这一门槛。

Method: 采用双钻石框架进行协同共创，与圭亚那和爱沙尼亚的大学生合作，映射痛点后对RSC v4.1底盘进行重设计：引入扭锁紧固件、卡扣接头和无工具维修闩锁等DfA/DfD干预措施。通过两项研究（面向开发者和首次构建者），使用SUS、NASA-TLX和组装时间等指标评估效果。

Result: 系统可用性评分从59.4（差）提升至89.4（优秀）；感知工作量（NASA-TLX）从4.29降至4.00；平均组装时间从21.4分钟降至13.7分钟（含初级学习者的学习效应）；首次构建者的方向提示和导航连续性被识别为下一个文档化前沿。

Conclusion: DfA/DfD干预措施在缩短组装时间之前就已改变了构建体验的感知方式；决定学生是否接受开源硬件的关键因素是感知工作量而非完成时间。

Abstract: Open-source social robots offer accessibility, repairability, and student empowerment, yet the build itself often presents a barrier. Existing platforms either ship pre-assembled, foreclosing hands-on learning, or expose students to unfamiliar fasteners, opaque wiring, and inaccessible service points that erode engagement. Whether targeted mechanical redesign can lower this barrier whilst maintaining structural integrity remains untested. Here we show that Design for Assembly (DfA) and Design for Disassembly (DfD) interventions reshape how a build feels before they shorten how long it takes. Working with university students in Guyana and Estonia, we applied the Double Diamond framework to co-create the Robot Study Companion (RSC) v4.1: mapping pain points, then redesigning its chassis around twist-lock fasteners, snap-fit joints, and tool-free service latches. Across two studies with developers and first-time builders, system usability climbed from Poor to Excellent (SUS 59.4 to 89.4), perceived workload trended downward (NASA-TLX 4.29 to 4.00), and mean assembly time trended downward (21.4 to 13.7 minutes, with juniors' learning effect), whilst orientation cues and navigation continuity for first-time builders emerged as the next documentation frontier. Perceived workload, not completion time, appears to govern whether students take up open hardware.

</details>


### [21] [Driving, Fast or Slow? Neuro-Symbolic Guidance for Motion Prediction in Multi-Modal Ground Mobility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15251)
*Simon Kohaut,Felix Divo,Julius Hahnewald,Benedict Flade,Julian Eggert,Kristian Kersting,Devendra Singh Dhami*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TraCS是一种神经符号框架，通过可解释的概率一阶逻辑增强黑盒运动预测模型，在Argoverse 2基准测试上持续提升最先进预测骨干网络的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动预测方法多为黑盒模型，缺乏对真实交通空间中行人与车辆行为约束（交通规则、行为规范）的显式编码，导致预测结果不够准确且难以解释。

Method: TraCS包含三个核心组件：(1) 代理代码生成流水线，将自然语言描述的交通规则桥接到概率运动预测；(2) 反应式数据流推理引擎，维护并高效更新场景演化的合规性景观；(3) 神经置信度评分，通过上下文感知的合规信号衰减防止过度自信的错误引导。

Result: 在Argoverse 2基准测试上，TraCS一致性地提升了多种最先进预测骨干网络的性能。

Conclusion: 概率符号合规推理是纯神经运动预测器的广泛适用且计算高效的补充方案，能够提升预测的准确性和可解释性。

Abstract: Accurate and interpretable motion prediction for heterogeneous traffic spaces, including pedestrians, bicycles, cars, and trucks, is essential for safe autonomous navigation. Nevertheless, state-of-the-art approaches remain predominantly black-box, lacking explicit encoding of the regulatory and behavioral constraints of real-world mobility. We propose Trajectory Compliance-Shaping (TraCS), a neuro-symbolic framework that augments existing black-box motion prediction backbones with interpretable and probabilistic first-order logic. To do so, TraCS employs an agentic code-generation pipeline to bridge the gap between natural-language descriptions of traffic regulations and probabilistic motion prediction. Furthermore, TraCS employs a reactive data-streaming inference engine that maintains and efficiently updates compliance landscapes as scenes evolve. To prevent TraCS from overconfidently steering the backbone's predictions in the wrong direction, we propose a neural confidence rating learned as a context-aware attenuation of the compliance signal. We demonstrate on the Argoverse 2 benchmark how TraCS consistently improves state-of-the-art prediction backbones, showing that probabilistic and symbolic compliance reasoning is a broadly applicable and computationally efficient complement to purely neural motion predictors.

</details>


### [22] [OSDAG: Online Scheduling for Efficient Multi-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15255)
*Thanh Nguyen Canh,Thang Tran Viet,Phuc Van Dinh,Xiem HoangVan,Nak Young Chong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出OSDAG框架，将LLM任务推理与DAG表示及在线调度结合，通过单次LLM调用生成依赖标注的任务图，再经由轻量级在线调度器实时分配任务，实现异构多机器人系统的高效协调，推理速度提升5-15倍，完工时间缩短最高38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的多机器人协调方法存在两大瓶颈：(1)执行过程中重复进行LLM推理导致延迟随机器人数量增长；(2)离线预提交调度迫使机器人闲置等待前序任务，即使存在可并行执行的独立任务。亟需一种既能保持高层推理灵活性又能实现低层高效调度的框架。

Method: OSDAG框架包含三个阶段：(1)单次LLM调用将自然语言指令分解为带依赖标注的任务DAG；(2)DAG编码任务间的优先约束和资源约束，暴露所有可并行任务；(3)轻量级在线调度器实时将就绪任务分配给空闲机器人，避免预提交调度的闲置问题。

Result: 在五个基准场景中，OSDAG相比对话式方法推理速度提升5-15倍；相较顺序基线方法，完工时间（makespan）减少最高38%；同时保持具有竞争力的任务成功率。在双臂操作任务的仿真与真实实验中均验证了方法有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: OSDAG通过将LLM推理与DAG在线调度解耦，有效解决了多机器人协调中推理延迟与调度低效两大关键问题，为异构多机器人系统执行复杂长时任务提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Coordinating heterogeneous multi-robot systems (MRS) for complex, long-horizon tasks requires both flexible high-level reasoning and efficient low-level scheduling. Existing LLM-based approaches address the reasoning side but introduce two critical bottlenecks: (1) repeated LLM inference during execution, which inflates latency with agent count, and (2) offline, pre-committed scheduling, which forces robots to idle while waiting for sequentially ordered predecessors even when independent work is available. This paper presents OSDAG, a novel framework that integrates LLM-based task reasoning with Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) representation and constraint-aware online scheduling. The LLM is invoked once to decompose a natural-language instruction into a dependency-annotated task graph, and a lightweight online scheduler then allocates ready tasks to idle agents in real time. The DAG representation encodes both precedence and resource constraints, ensuring correctness while exposing all available parallelism. Experiments across five benchmark scenarios demonstrate that OSDAG achieves 5-15x faster reasoning time compared to dialogue-based methods, reduces makespan by up to 38% over sequential baselines, and maintains competitive success rates. Both simulation and real-world experiments on dual-arm manipulation tasks validate the effectiveness and practicality of the proposed approach for efficient multi-robot coordination. The website and resources are available at http://thanhnguyencanh.github.io/LLM_DAG4MultiRobot

</details>


### [23] [Acting While Understanding: Asynchronous Semantic-Action Decoupling for Real-Time Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15285)
*Shenhao Yan,Ge Wang,Qi Liu,Weilin Meng,Jiahao Yang,Chengsi Yao,Fan Feng,Xiaoguang Ma,Yiming Zhao,Yatong Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种异步语义-动作解耦框架，将VLA模型的语义理解与动作生成分离，通过低频语义更新和高频动作推理实现高频闭环控制，无需完整模型推理，在LIBERO和真实机器人上达到35.6 Hz动作推理吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型全模型推理计算成本高，难以部署在低延迟、高频的闭环控制场景中。

Method: 提出异步语义-动作解耦框架：低频理解模块异步更新可复用语义条件，高频动作模块持续输出控制动作；引入历史动作条件化和时间错位训练以缓解语义与执行状态的时序不匹配问题。

Result: 在LIBERO上基于π_{0.5}和UniVLA，以及使用UniVLA的真实机器人部署中，实现了高达35.6 Hz的服务端动作模块推理吞吐量。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一条低侵入路径，无需以控制频率运行完整VLA推理即可实现高频闭环控制。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) have demonstrated strong task understanding and generalization in robotic manipulation, yet the high computational cost of full-model inference limits their deployment in low-latency, high-frequency closed-loop control. We propose an asynchronous semantic-action decoupling framework that separates semantic understanding from action generation along the internal semantic-action interface of existing VLAs, without redesigning the vision-language backbone or introducing an external planner. A low-frequency understanding module asynchronously updates reusable semantic conditions, while a high-frequency action module continuously outputs control actions without repeatedly invoking the full model. To mitigate the temporal mismatch between stale semantics and the current execution state, we further introduce historical action conditioning and time-misalignment training, which provide short-horizon execution context and improve feedback control robustness under stale semantic conditions. Experiments on LIBERO with $π_{0.5}$ and UniVLA, together with real-robot deployment using UniVLA, show that the proposed framework achieves up to 35.6 Hz server-side action-module inference throughput and offers a low-intrusion path to high-frequency closed-loop control without running full VLA inference at control rate.

</details>


### [24] [Covariance-Regulated Recursive Koopman Learning for Nonlinear Systems with Uncertain Time-Varying Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15317)
*Weibin Gu,Chen Yang,Lu Shi,Chao Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对离线模型在时变动态环境下失效的问题，本文提出协方差调节递归Koopman学习框架（CR-RKL），通过误差死区门控和常量迹归一化两种策略，解决了递归Koopman估计中的协方差爆炸和参数冻结问题，在非完整轮式机器人和扑翼微型飞行器上验证了数值稳定且准确的在线建模与MPC控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线训练的自主机器人模型在遇到训练分布外的时变动动力学时往往失效，而现有Koopman算子的递归估计方法存在数值脆弱性：使用指数遗忘时在低激励条件下会出现协方差饱和（风车效应），不使用遗忘则会导致增益消失、参数冻结。

Method: 提出协方差调节递归Koopman学习（CR-RKL）框架，包含两种互补策略：（1）误差死区门控——根据预测误差大小选择性更新，防止异常值导致的协方差爆炸；（2）常量迹归一化——保持协方差矩阵迹为常数，在防止参数冻结的同时保持不确定性的几何结构。

Result: 在存在车轮滑移和Stribeck摩擦的非完整差动驱动轮式机器人以及26克仿蝴蝶扑翼微型飞行器上验证，CR-RKL实现了数值稳定且准确的在线建模；嵌入模型预测控制后，在不确定时变动环境下保持可靠的跟踪性能。

Conclusion: CR-RKL框架有效克服了递归Koopman学习的数值不稳定问题，为时变动环境下自主机器人的在线建模与控制提供了一种鲁棒解决方案。

Abstract: Offline models for autonomous robots often fail under time-varying dynamics outside their training distribution. Koopman operator theory offers a linear representation of nonlinear dynamics via lifting, but its transition to real-time recursive estimation may suffer numerical vulnerabilities: covariance windup under low excitation when using exponential forgetting, and vanishing gain without forgetting. This paper introduces a Covariance-Regulated Recursive Koopman Learning (CR-RKL) framework with two complementary strategies--error dead-zone gating and constant-trace normalization--each independently capable of preventing covariance explosion and parameter freezing, with the latter additionally preserving the geometric structure of uncertainty. Validated on a non-holonomic differential-drive robot with wheel slip and Stribeck friction and on a 26-gram butterfly-inspired flapping-wing micro aerial vehicle, CR-RKL achieves numerically stable and accurate online modeling, and when embedded in model predictive control, it maintains reliable tracking performance under uncertain, time-varying dynamics.

</details>


### [25] [SimWeaver: Zero-Shot RGB Sim-to-Real for Deformable Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15338)
*Wenkang Hu,Haoran Wang,Yitong Li,Liu Liu,Mengao Zhao,Lai Jiang,Xincheng Tang,Junhang Wei,Zhengjie Shu,Zhendong Wang,Zhizhong Su,Huamin Wang,Ruigang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SimWeaver提出了一套完整的RGB仿真到真实迁移框架，仅用200条模拟演示即可训练零样本VLA策略，在5种可变形物体操作任务上达到平均91%的真实世界成功率，无需遥操作或任务级校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形物体操作的RGB仿真到真实迁移仍是一个未解决的问题，现有方法通常需要真实世界微调或遥操作数据采集，成本高昂且难以扩展。

Method: SimWeaver包含四个核心组件：1) SimWeaver-Sim: 基于可靠物理测量的仿真器；2) SimWeaver-Asset: 支持单图生成的可扩展资产框架；3) SimWeaver-Syn: 确定性拓扑感知轨迹合成器；4) SimWeaver-Real: 包含ISP感知光度增强的仿真到真实协议。

Result: 在5种可变形任务上达到80%以上单任务成功率，平均91%的真实世界成功率；在丝织物抓取任务中，仿真训练策略在视觉分布偏移下达到100%成功率，而真实数据基线降至9-70%，且每个轨迹成本低两个数量级。

Conclusion: SimWeaver证明了无需真实世界微调即可实现可变形物体操作RGB策略的仿真到真实迁移，为可变形操作任务提供了一种低成本、高可靠性的训练范式。

Abstract: RGB sim-to-real for deformable manipulation has remained largely unsolved without real-world fine-tuning. We present SimWeaver, which trains zero-shot RGB VLA policies on 200 simulated demonstrations per task, reaching above 80% per-task and 91% average real-world success across 5 diverse deformable tasks including plastic-bag manipulation, without teleoperation or per-task calibration. SimWeaver combines a reliable measurement-backed simulator (SimWeaver-Sim) with an extensible asset framework supporting single-image generation(SimWeaver-Asset), a deterministic topology-aware trajectory synthesizer (SimWeaver-Syn), and a sim-to-real protocol with ISP-aware photometric augmentation (SimWeaver-Real). On silk grasping, the sim-trained policy reaches 100% under visual distribution shifts where real-data baselines drop to 9-70%, at two orders of magnitude lower per-trajectory cost. We will release SimWeaver and a representative asset subset. Project page: https://simweaver.github.io/

</details>


### [26] [A Hybrid Model-Based and Model-Free Framework for Active Multi-View Viewpoint Optimization in Sonar Target Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15373)
*Yongkyoon Park,Jane Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合模型驱动与数据驱动的混合框架，用于前视声纳下的主动多视角目标识别。采用CNN提供观测似然，Radon变换估计视角朝向，PPO学习视角选择策略，在海洋垃圾数据集上实现了识别精度与感知效率的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统主动视角选择方法（如POMDP树搜索）计算开销大，难以实时部署；而纯数据驱动方法缺乏对视角信息的显式建模。需要一种既能利用数据驱动优势又能保持计算效率的混合方法。

Method: 1) CNN提供数据驱动的观测似然；2) Radon变换用于朝向估计，实现视角感知且无需角度标注；3) PPO代理在训练阶段基于信息增益奖励学习信念感知的视角选择策略；4) 部署时仅需CNN信念更新即可实时选择视角，无需POMDP在线搜索。

Result: 在海洋垃圾前视声纳数据集上，所提方法在达到竞争性识别精度的同时，减少了感知步数与运动成本，优于基于模型的基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提混合框架成功将模型驱动的视角选择策略与数据驱动的识别能力相结合，既避免了POMDP在线搜索的计算负担，又实现了高效的主动多视角目标识别。

Abstract: This paper presents a hybrid model-based and model-free framework for active multi-view target recognition using forward-looking sonar. A convolutional neural network (CNN) provides data-driven observation likelihoods, while Radon-based orientation estimation enables viewpoint-aware sensing without requiring angle annotations. During training, an information-gain-based reward guides a Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) agent to learn a belief-aware viewpoint selection policy offline. At deployment, the learned policy performs real-time viewpoint selection using only CNN-based belief updates, eliminating the need for computationally expensive online POMDP tree search. Experiments on a marine-debris forward-looking sonar dataset demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves competitive recognition accuracy while reducing sensing steps and motion cost compared to model-based baselines.

</details>

*Sima Ashayer,Austin Haris,Mina Sartipi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于CARLA和VISSIM的联合仿真框架，用于包含约15个信号交叉口的城市走廊（田纳西州查塔努加市MLK大道），通过双向步进同步接口实现VISSIM的微观交通逻辑与CARLA高保真3D渲染的集成，并在约100辆车和100名行人的高峰负载下验证了信号同步、车-人交互及混合控制稳定性的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微观交通仿真（如VISSIM）缺乏高保真3D感知渲染能力，而高保真仿真器（如CARLA）又难以直接处理大规模多交叉口走廊的复杂交通信号控制逻辑。需要建立一种能够兼顾交通逻辑精确性和视觉感知真实性的联合仿真框架，以支持走廊级的交通研究与验证。

Method: 构建CARLA-VISSIM双向步进同步联合仿真框架：集成CARLA 0.10.0（Unreal Engine 5）与PTV VISSIM 2026，通过显式的Actor所有权、镜像生命周期管理、坐标系对齐和按Actor最新状态更新策略，实现VISSIM控制的交通流与CARLA控制的自动驾驶车辆之间的稳定交互。利用LiDAR高程模型和RoadRunner高精地图确保两个仿真器使用一致的地形精确道路几何。

Result: 走廊级案例研究表明：该框架能够实现一致的信号灯镜像、同步的车-人交互以及稳定的混合控制运行（约100辆车和100名行人）；成功捕获了MLK街5个信号交叉口及其上下游交叉口的交互行为，揭示了多交叉口走廊特有的同步挑战。

Conclusion: 该MLK走廊联合仿真框架为验证跨仿真器一致性提供了有效的试验平台，所提出的架构能够支持可靠且可用于感知的走廊级交通联合仿真研究。

Abstract: This paper presents an implemented CARLA-VISSIM co-simulation framework for an urban corridor comprising approximately fifteen connected intersections centered on Martin Luther King Jr. Boulevard in Chattanooga, Tennessee. The system integrates CARLA 0.10.0 Unreal Engine 5 with PTV VISSIM 2026 through a bidirectional, step-synchronized interface that couples VISSIM's microscopic vehicle, pedestrian, and signal-controller logic with CARLA's high-fidelity 3D rendering. A LiDAR-derived elevation model and RoadRunner-based High Definition (HD) map provide terrain-accurate road geometry deployed consistently across both simulators. The framework incorporates explicit actor ownership, mirrored lifecycle management, coordinate reconciliation, and a latest-state-per-actor update policy, enabling stable interaction between VISSIM-controlled traffic and a CARLA-controlled ego vehicle. A corridor-scale case study demonstrates consistent traffic-signal mirroring, synchronized vehicle-pedestrian interactions, and stable mixed-authority operation under peak loads of approximately 100 vehicles and 100 pedestrians. The deployment captures the interaction of the five signalized intersections along MLK Street and their connecting upstream and downstream intersections, revealing synchronization challenges unique to multi-intersection corridors. Results indicate that this MLK-centered corridor provides an effective testbed for verifying cross-simulator consistency and that the proposed architecture supports reliable, perception-ready co-simulation for corridor-level traffic studies.

</details>


### [28] [A Bilateral Teleoperation Framework for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15434)
*Stefano Dalla Gasperina,Dong Ho Kang,Haiyun Zhang,Aldo Galvan,Job D. Ramirez,Aaron Kim,Mark Helwig,Kazuto Yokoyama,Takahisa Ueno,Tetsuya Narita,Ann Majewicz-Fey,Ashish D. Deshpande,Luis Sentis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模块化双边遥操作框架，集成了操作者侧输入接口与机器人侧灵巧手及柔顺臂，支持位置重定向、差分臂控、多尺度力反馈与共享控制，并通过真实世界操作任务验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧遥操作需要精确的臂-手协调、低延迟反馈以及在真实接触丰富环境中的鲁棒交互，现有系统在跨具身匹配、力反馈粒度与共享控制方面仍存在不足，亟需一个统一的模块化框架来解决这些问题。

Method: 采用模块化双边架构，集成位置基手部重定向、差分臂控、多尺度触觉反馈与共享控制机制，在统一控制框架下实现操作者与机器人侧灵巧手及柔顺臂的协调。

Result: 通过真实世界的灵巧操作任务验证了框架的有效性，展示了协调的臂-手控制与接触感知交互能力。

Conclusion: 该平台不仅提供了一个实用的遥操作系统，还为未来基于示教学习的研究提供了高质量演示数据采集的基础，并揭示了跨具身不匹配、触觉反馈粒度与共享控制等关键设计洞见。

Abstract: Dexterous teleoperation requires precise arm-hand coordination, low-latency feedback, and robust interaction in real-world contact-rich environments. This paper presents a modular bilateral teleoperation framework that integrates operator-side input interfaces with a robot-side dexterous hand and compliant robotic arm in a unified control architecture. The system supports position-based hand retargeting, differential arm control, multi-scale haptic feedback, and shared control for stable manipulation. We validate the framework through a real-world dexterous manipulation task, highlighting coordinated arm-hand control and contact-aware interaction. Beyond feasibility, we identify key design insights related to cross-embodiment mismatch, haptic feedback granularity, and shared control. The proposed platform provides a practical teleoperation system and a foundation for collecting high-quality demonstrations for future learning-from-demonstration research.

</details>


### [29] [Learning Context-Aware Neural ODE Dynamics for Adaptive Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15469)
*Shao-Yi Yu,Jen-Wei Wang,Maya Horii,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Vikas Garg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经常微分方程的环境感知动力学模型，通过两阶段训练从状态-动作历史中推断环境因素，实现模型预测控制下的自适应。在四旋翼（仿真）、Sphero BOLT机器人和Fanuc机械臂（实物）上验证了该方法对时空变化环境的适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不确定和动态变化的环境中，机器人系统面临接触条件、气动效应和外部干扰的变化，而基于模型的控制需要能够适应这些变化的动力学模型，尤其在无法直接获取完整环境信息时。

Method: 提出基于神经常微分方程（Neural ODE）的环境感知动力学模型，通过两阶段训练过程从状态-动作历史中推断环境因素，并集成到模型预测控制（MPC）框架中。

Result: 在仿真的四旋翼、实物的Sphero BOLT机器人和Fanuc机械臂上进行了验证，结果表明该方法能有效适应不同任务中时空变化的环境变化。

Conclusion: 该方法使机器人系统能够在有限环境信息条件下实现自适应控制，有效应对时空变化的环境干扰，并具备跨平台通用性。

Abstract: Robotic systems deployed in uncertain and dynamically changing environments often face variations in contact conditions, aerodynamic effects, and external disturbances that challenge reliable control. To remain effective under model-based control, these systems require dynamics models that can adapt to such changes, especially when direct access to complete environmental information is limited. To enable adaptability and facilitate integration with model predictive control, we propose a context-aware dynamics model based on neural ordinary differential equations, which infers environmental factors from state-action histories using a two-phase training procedure. We validate the approach across diverse robotic platforms, including a quadrotor in simulation, as well as a Sphero BOLT robot and a Fanuc manipulator in real-world experiments. The results demonstrate that our method effectively adapts to temporally and spatially varying environmental changes across different tasks. Videos are available at https://youtu.be/PY0sNyF2rqE , and the source code is available at https://github.com/syyu410-yu/context-aware-neural-ode-control.git .

</details>


### [30] [FARM: Find Anything using Relational Spatial Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15476)
*Siming He,Leo Huang,Adam Lilja,Fabio Hubel,Jonas Frey,Marco Pavone,S. Shankar Sastry,Jitendra Malik,Claire Tomlin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FARM是一个实时构建开放词汇、对象级关系空间记忆的系统，通过视觉-语言描述符和几何信息，利用VLM解析查询并显式约束空间关系，在44k语言查询上相比之前方法Recall@5和Recall@10分别提升164%和224%，Accuracy@1提升35%，并在四足机器人上实现了闭环部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在物体密集环境中，对象级记忆不足以满足按需检索特定实例的需求，因为用户常通过参考物和周围对象的空间关系（如"飞镖盘下方、海报左侧的高台灯"）来指代目标，需要支持语义、外观和空间谓词检索的关系空间记忆系统。

Method: FARM以5-10Hz实时构建紧凑的开放词汇对象级记忆，包含几何信息、视觉-语言描述符和视角证据；查询时使用VLM解析查询并评分视觉证据，同时通过对象符号和关系谓词显式约束空间条件。

Result: 在44k语言查询（涵盖67个室内外场景，面积15-15,000 m²）上，Recall@5和Recall@10分别提升164%和224%，最终VLM重排序阶段使Accuracy@1提升35%，且系统实时运行。在四足机器人上使用机载传感器和计算实现了闭环部署。

Conclusion: 对VLM的结构化使用（显式空间约束+视觉证据评分）比端到端推理帧历史或场景图上下文能实现更准确、鲁棒的检索，证明关系空间记忆在机器人按需物体检索任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Robots operating in homes, warehouses, and other object-rich environments need memory systems that can find specific object instances on demand. Object-level memory alone is often insufficient: scenes contain many plausibly matching objects, and users refer to the target through relations to landmarks and surrounding objects (e.g. ``the tall lamp below the dartboard and to the left of the poster''), demanding a relational spatial memory that supports retrieval through semantic, appearance, and spatial predicates over objects. To achieve this, we present FARM (Find Anything using Relational Spatial Memory), which builds, in real time at 5-10 Hz, a compact, open-vocabulary, object-level memory with geometry, visual-language descriptors, and viewpoint evidence. At query time, FARM uses VLMs to parse the query and score visual evidence, while grounding spatial constraints explicitly through object symbols and relational predicates. This structured use of VLMs enables more accurate and robust retrieval than end-to-end reasoning over frame histories or scene-graph context. In experiments on 44k language queries spanning 67 indoor and outdoor scenes, ranging from 15 to 15,000 m^2, FARM improves Recall@5 and Recall@10 over prior methods by 164% and 224%, and a final VLM reranking stage improves Accuracy@1 by 35%, while running in real time. We further demonstrate closed-loop deployment on a quadrupedal robot using onboard sensors and compute.

</details>


### [31] [FD-SLAM: Fast Dense Radar-Inertial SLAM with Frequency-Domain Loop Closure and Pose Graph Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15491)
*Nader J. Abu-Alrub,Nathir A. Rawashdeh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FD-SLAM是一种快速密集雷达-惯性SLAM系统，通过频域闭环检测和位姿图优化，在视觉退化环境下实现自主地面车辆的稳健定位，在公开数据集上取得与最先进方法相当的精度，且计算效率满足实时要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扫描雷达噪声大、扫描率低，且其测量值难以在长轨迹上可靠匹配，现有方法在视觉退化环境下的SLAM性能有限，需要更鲁棒且高效的雷达SLAM方案。

Method: 在密集雷达-惯性里程计基础上，采用紧凑频域极坐标描述子进行闭环候选检索，并通过时间滤波、相位相关筛选、扫描对齐相似度及几何一致性检验构成多阶段验证流水线；将验证后的闭环作为非序列约束加入SE(2)位姿图优化中。

Result: 在公开数据集上使用KITTI标准评估指标，FD-SLAM改进了FD-RIO基线，性能与当前最先进雷达SLAM方法相当，在多个驾驶轨迹上旋转精度优越；雷达-惯性前端在仅CPU环境下运行速率高于雷达采样率，闭环检测与图优化适合并行后台执行。

Conclusion: FD-SLAM通过频域闭环策略有效解决了雷达测量噪声大、长轨迹匹配困难的问题，在计算效率和定位精度之间取得了良好平衡，为视觉退化环境下的自主导航提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Radar SLAM is attractive for autonomous ground vehicles operating in visually degraded environments, however, scanning radars are noisy, have low scanning rates, and their measurements are challenging to match reliably over long trajectories. This paper presents FD-SLAM, a fast dense radar-inertial SLAM system that extends dense radar-inertial odometry with frequency-domain loop closure and pose graph optimization. The proposed method preserves an image-like structure of scanning radar measurements by using a compact frequency-domain polar descriptor for loop-candidate retrieval and a multi-stage verification pipeline based on temporal filtering, phase-correlation screening, scan-alignment similarity, and geometric consistency checks. Verified loop closures are added as non-sequential constraints in an SE(2) pose graph together with radar-inertial odometry factors. FD-SLAM is evaluated on a publicly available dataset using standard KITTI evaluation metrics. The results show that FD-SLAM improves FD-RIO baseline, achieves competitive performance against current state-of-the-art radar SLAM methods, and provides favorable rotational accuracy across multiple evaluated driving trajectories. Runtime analysis further indicates that the radar-inertial front-end operates above the radar sampling rate on a CPU-only setup, while loop closure detection and graph optimization remain suitable for parallel background execution.

</details>


### [32] [Understanding and Modeling Perceived Cognitive and Physical Strain Dynamics for Planning-Oriented Human-Robot Collaboration in Prefabricated Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15494)
*Yifan Wang,Bo Xiao,Shane T. Mueller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对预制建筑中的人机协作，通过重复工作-休息实验，建立了基于经验数据的感知应变累积与恢复模型，发现认知应变累积符合线性混合效应模型，而休息恢复呈现非线性衰减，为未来考虑工人状态的任务分配与调度提供了规划导向的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规划模型在预制建筑人机协作中常采用简化的疲劳、工作负荷或恢复假设，缺乏领域特定且基于经验证据的感知应变演变机制理解，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过可控重复工作-休息实验，使用RSME量表评估认知应变、Borg RPE量表评估身体应变；比较线性和指数函数形式，并采用混合效应模型分析协作条件、会话效应及个体间差异。

Result: 认知应变累积最佳拟合线性混合效应模型，而休息阶段恢复呈现非线性衰减模式。

Conclusion: 所建立的规划导向模型可为未来考虑工人状态的任务分配与调度研究提供依据。

Abstract: Human-robot collaboration (HRC) in prefabricated construction requires planning approaches that consider not only productivity but also time-dependent worker states during repeated work and rest. Existing planning models often rely on simplified assumptions about fatigue, workload, or recovery, with limited domain-specific empirical evidence on how perceived strain evolves. This study develops an empirically grounded, planning-oriented approach to characterize perceived strain accumulation and recovery in prefabricated construction HRC. A controlled repeated work-rest experiment assessed perceived cognitive and physical strain using the Rating Scale for Mental Effort and Borg's Rating of Perceived Exertion. Linear and exponential functional forms were evaluated, followed by mixed-effects modeling to examine collaborative conditions, session effects, and inter-individual variability. Results indicate that cognitive strain accumulation is best represented by a linear mixed-effects model, whereas rest-phase recovery follows nonlinear decay. The resulting planning-oriented models may inform future human-state-aware task allocation and scheduling research.

</details>


### [33] [Reinforcement Learning-Guided Retrieval with Soft Fusion for Robust Multimodal Imitation Learning under Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15514)
*Hassan Ismkhan,Hamid Bouchahcia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RL4IL方法，利用强化学习从训练库中检索最相关的专家演示，以处理机器人任务中传感器缺失（如摄像头故障）的情况，在LIBERO基准测试上显著优于现有模仿学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实机器人部署中传感器可能故障、遮挡或完全丢失，而现有方法通常假设所有输入模态永久可用，缺乏对缺失模态场景的鲁棒处理能力。

Method: RL4IL包含两个核心组件：(1) 用PPO在BFS候选集上训练的强化学习策略，用于排序候选演示；(2) 软交叉注意力融合头聚合动作信号。模态缺失时，专用检索策略从训练库中寻找"捐赠"演示，软插补头通过交叉注意力重构缺失嵌入，无需重新训练。

Result: 在三个LIBERO基准上，RL4IL在传感器丢失条件下显著优于最先进的模仿学习方法，且无需策略网络训练。

Conclusion: RL4IL通过强化学习引导的演示检索与软注意力机制，有效解决了机器人学习中传感器缺失的鲁棒性问题，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Robotic systems perceive the world through multiple input modalities -- including visual camera streams and natural language instructions -- and must select appropriate actions based on these signals. However, assuming the permanent availability of all input devices is unrealistic, as sensors may fail, become occluded, or drop out entirely during deployment. Robust handling of such missing-modality scenarios is therefore essential for real-world robot operation. This paper introduces RL4IL, a reinforcement learning guided method for imitation learning that selects the most suitable action for a given observation by identifying the most relevant expert demonstrations from a training library. A reinforcement learning policy, trained via Proximal Policy Optimisation over Breadth-First Search candidate sets, ranks candidate demonstrations and a soft cross-attention fusion head aggregates their action signals to produce the final prediction. When a modality is missing at inference time, a dedicated per-modality RL retrieval policy identifies donor demonstrations from the training library, and a soft imputation head reconstructs the missing embedding via cross-attention over the top-ranked donors -- without requiring any retraining of the system. Experiments on three LIBERO benchmark suites demonstrate that RL4IL substantially outperforms state-of-the-art imitation learning methods under sensor dropout conditions, while requiring no policy network training. The code can be found at https://github.com/h-ismkhan/Reinforcement-Learning-via-kNN-for-Robotic-Learning-with-Missing-Camera

</details>

Result: 在结构不同的多种灵巧手上实验表明，标定后的接触反馈能够实现可迁移的柔顺抓取，学习到的基元可在长序列操作流程中复用。

Abstract: Dexterous grasping depends on contact regulation, not motion alone. Stable manipulation requires fingers to maintain appropriate object loading as contacts slip, deform, or become visually occluded. Existing cross-embodiment dexterous policies unify motion through retargeted hand poses or latent actions, but force feedback remains tied to each hand's sensing and actuation, limiting transfer. Motion intent is represented in a shared hand-pose latent, while each hand's effort signal is calibrated through system identification into physical joint torque in N.m. These torques are mapped to fingertip forces and compact per-finger load descriptors, giving the policy comparable observations of where the hand should move and how the object is loaded. Using this interface, a flow-matching visuomotor policy is trained on vision, proprioception, and calibrated contact, with structured visual masking that encourages reliance on force under grasp-relevant occlusion. The same calibrated signal drives a hybrid force-position controller for demonstration collection and execution, keeping force targets consistent across training and deployment. Experiments across structurally different hands show that calibrated contact feedback enables transferable compliant grasping, with learned primitives reusable in long-horizon manipulation pipelines.

</details>

*Ruofei Bai,Jie Chen,Yuxin Cai,Jun Li,Wei-Yun Yau,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Roken，一个统一的扩散Transformer模型，将每个机器人表示为离散token，通过自注意力机制实现机器人间交互，并引入基于贝叶斯定理的辅助任务进行多尺度时空监督，从而以前馈方式直接生成满足安全性和连通性约束的多机器人协调轨迹。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在多种杂乱环境下的连通性约束目标导航任务中，Roken生成协调多机器人轨迹的成功率高于生成训练数据集的基线方法；训练混合团队规模后表现出良好的可扩展性，并对未见或部分观测环境具有泛化能力。

Abstract: The success of generative models in language and visual generation has inspired extensive applications to generative robot planning. However, most existing works either focus on single-robot planning, or generate multi-robot trajectories in a sequential manner with iterative post-processing to resolve inter-robot conflicts. In this work, we investigate whether coordinated multi-robot trajectories, as a special spatiotemporal distribution, can be learned and generated with a generative model in a feed-forward manner. We propose Robots as Tokens (Roken), a unified diffusion transformer that directly generates multi-robot trajectories that satisfy both (individual) safety and (global) connectivity constraints. The core design of Roken is to represent each robot as a discrete token, allowing them to naturally interact with each other through self-attention, and cross-attend to map tokens for environment layouts. We further introduce several auxiliary tasks based on Bayes' theorem to provide multi-scale spatial-temporal supervision for efficient learning of the conditional distribution. In training, Roken absorbs diverse expert trajectories from different team sizes. During inference, Roken behaves as a versatile multi-robot planner that can handle single-robot planning, coordinated multi-robot trajectory generation, and conditional trajectory generation by fixing some robot tokens as conditions. Experiments in diverse cluttered environments show that Roken can generate coordinated multi-robot trajectories to perform connectivity-constrained goal navigation tasks with high success rates, outperforming the baseline method used to generate the training dataset. Roken also demonstrates good scalability after training with mixed team sizes, and shows generalization to unseen or partially observed environments, verifying its potential to learn from diverse data and perform versatile tasks.

</details>
