Abstract: This paper is concerned with the differentially private consensus problem for discrete-time multi-agent systems with communication delays. The purpose of the paper is to achieve differentially private consensus for such systems while protecting the entire delayed initial histories of all agents. A novel adjacency relation for delayed histories is introduced, and a Laplace-noise-based privacy mechanism is developed, where the noise variance is allowed to vary with time and even increase. By using the difference resolvent function method, decay estimates for the fundamental solutions of the delayed difference equations are derived. Based on these estimates and a backstepping technique, mean square weak consensus, mean square strong consensus, and almost sure strong consensus are established. The estimates for the fundamental solutions are also used to derive an explicit sensitivity bound. Furthermore, a constructive parameter design is provided to achieve a prescribed infinite-horizon $ε^\star$-differential privacy level. Numerical simulations illustrate the theoretical results.

</details>


### [110] [Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability-Based Safe Reinforcement Learning for Emergency Collision Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15311)
*Yuhong Jiang,Shiyue Zhao,Junzhi Zhang,Junfeng Zhang,Xinhan Li,Shijie Zhao,Chengkun He*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Hamilton-Jacobi可达性运动安全集的强化学习框架，通过融合几何碰撞裕度和底盘稳定性极限构建统一安全函数，并利用离线极端驾驶数据近似计算运动安全集，将其作为连续安全代价嵌入约束马尔可夫决策过程，采用PID-Lagrangian策略优化进行自适应约束调节，在低附着系数避障场景中实现了更高的目标到达率、更平滑的避障 maneuvers 和更大的统一安全裕度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端驾驶条件下的紧急避撞需要同时考虑障碍物距离和车辆动态稳定性的安全关键控制，但现有方法往往依赖瞬时或局部安全评估，缺乏对未来时域的前瞻性安全保障。

Method: 1) 构建结合几何碰撞裕度和底盘稳定性极限的统一符号安全函数；2) 通过可达性分析扩展为有限时域运动安全集；3) 利用离线极端驾驶数据近似运动安全集以避免网格求解计算负担；4) 将运动安全集作为连续安全代价嵌入约束马尔可夫决策过程；5) 采用PID-Lagrangian策略优化自适应调节拉格朗日乘子以强制执行安全约束。

Result: 在低附着系数避障场景的仿真和实车实验中，所提方法相比基线方法实现了更高的目标到达率、更平滑的避障动作，并保持了更大的统一安全裕度。

Conclusion: 基于HJ可达性运动安全集的强化学习框架能够为极端驾驶条件下的紧急避撞提供前瞻性安全保障，有效平衡了避障性能与车辆动态稳定性。

Abstract: Emergency collision avoidance under extreme driving conditions demands safety-critical control that accounts for both obstacle proximity and vehicle dynamic stability over a future time horizon, yet existing methods often rely on instantaneous or local safety evaluations. This paper proposes a safe reinforcement learning framework guided by a Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) reachability based motion safety set that provides forward-looking safety supervision for constrained policy optimization. Specifically, a unified signed safety function is formulated by combining geometric collision margins and chassis stability limits, and is then extended through reachability analysis into a finite-horizon motion safety set that characterizes whether safety can be maintained under future vehicle state evolution. To enable practical computation, the motion safety set is approximated from offline extreme driving data, mitigating the computational burden of grid-based HJ solvers. The learned motion safety set is then embedded as a continuous safety cost into a constrained Markov decision process, and a PID-Lagrangian policy optimization scheme is employed to adaptively regulate the Lagrange multiplier for safety constraint enforcement. Simulation and real-vehicle experiments on low-adhesion obstacle-avoidance scenarios demonstrate that the proposed method achieves higher goal-reaching rates, produces smoother avoidance maneuvers, and maintains larger unified safety margins than baseline methods.

</details>


### [111] [Robust Conformal CBF and CLF Controllers via Iterative Policy Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15366)
*Omid Mirzaeedodangeh,Eliot Shekhtman,Nikolai Matni,Lars Lindemann*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于对抗性鲁棒共形预测的迭代框架，通过在策略更新中量化分布转移预算，实现了鲁棒CLF/CBF策略在跨回合中的稳定性与安全性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将共形预测的误差界嵌入到鲁棒CLF/CBF框架中，但因闭环轨迹分布偏移（即部署策略与训练数据分布的差异），无法保留稳定性/安全性保证。

Method: 1) 采用对抗性鲁棒共形预测来应对分布偏移；2) 通过闭环轨迹灵敏度分析量化分布转移预算，控制模型误差在策略更新中的增长；3) 推导出CP界的隐式和显式更新规则，形成迭代式回合制框架。

Result: 在三个案例研究中验证了算法的收敛性，首次为鲁棒共形CBF/CLF策略提供了稳定性/安全性保证。

Conclusion: 该工作是首个在鲁棒共形CBF/CLF框架中同时提供稳定性与安全性保证的方法，有效地将共形预测的不确定性量化与基于学习的控制理论结合。

Abstract: Conformal prediction (CP) has been used to obtain probabilistic bounds on the error between a learned dynamics model and the true but unknown system. Such CP bounds can then be embedded into robust control Lyapunov function (CLF) and control barrier function (CBF) frameworks. However, such an approach does not retain stability/safety guarantees because of the distribution shift between the closed-loop trajectory distribution under the deployed CLF/CBF policy and the trajectory distribution from which the CP bound and its guarantees were derived. To address this issue, we propose an episodic framework that iteratively updates the robust conformal CLF/CBF policy while maintaining stability/safety guarantees across episodes. We achieve this by (1) using adversarially robust conformal prediction, and (2) quantifying a distribution shift budget that allows us to control how much the model error can increase across policy updates. This distribution shift budget is derived via a closed-loop trajectory sensitivity analysis, yielding an implicit and an explicit update rule for the CP bound. We analyze convergence of our algorithm, which we demonstrate on three case studies. To the best of our knowledge, these are the first results that provide stability/safety guarantees for robust conformal CBF/CLF policies.

</details>


### [112] [Data Center Life Cycle Co-Design Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15408)
*Shrenik Jadhav,Vidhyashree Nagaraju,Zheng Liu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个集成框架，用于优化液体冷却超级计算机冷却回路的分区数量与分配方案，综合考虑运营能耗、隐含碳和可靠性。以Frontier超算为案例，在611种可能配置中找到最优的2子回路方案（14+11单元），相比实际采用的4子回路方案，7年周期内节省50.2吨CO₂当量和10万美元成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液冷超算的冷却系统通常采用并行子回路结构，但子回路数量与冷却分配单元（CDU）的分配方案在设计建造阶段即固定，缺乏针对此规模设施的系统性优化。随着电网脱碳，隐含碳占设施全生命周期排放比重增大，不必要的子回路成本更难合理化。

Method: 构建集成框架，包含：(1) 基于序列最小二乘规划（SLSQP）的验证控制优化器驱动的运营能耗模型；(2) 基于物料清单的隐含碳核算；(3) 基于每子回路可靠性模型的预期非计划停机时间分析。将框架应用于Frontier超算的25个CDU划分为2至6个子回路的全部611种方案，进行全生命周期成本与碳排放评估。

Result: 最优配置为2个子回路分别容纳14和11个CDU，7年周期内产生3,320.7吨CO₂当量和399万美元成本，相比实际4子回路配置节省50.2吨CO₂和10万美元。该最优解在15种单因素灵敏度分析中均保持在帕累托前沿上。半解析决策规则推广后预测：Aurora需要4个子回路，El Capitan需要2个，LUMI需要1个。当可靠性作为硬约束时，Frontier的4子回路部署与约束最优一致。

Conclusion: 液冷超算冷却系统的子回路数量与CDU分配存在显著的优化空间，减少子回路数量可降低生命周期成本与碳排放，且不影响可靠性约束下的最优性。提出的半解析决策规则可推广至其他超算设施，为未来液冷超算的冷却系统设计提供理论依据。

Abstract: Liquid cooled supercomputers dissipate tens of megawatts of waste heat through cooling plants organized as parallel subloops that serve coolant distribution units. The number of subloops and the assignment of units to them are design decisions fixed at construction, yet they have not been systematically optimized for facilities at this scale. As electricity grids decarbonize, embodied carbon becomes a larger share of facility life cycle emissions and the cost of an unnecessary subloop becomes harder to justify. We present a framework that integrates operational energy from a validated control optimizer based on sequential least squares programming, embodied carbon from a bill of materials, and expected unplanned downtime from a per subloop reliability model. The framework is applied to the Frontier supercomputer, evaluating all 611 ways of partitioning its 25 coolant distribution units into two through six subloops. The life cycle cost and carbon optimum is found at two subloops holding 14 and 11 units, achieving 3,320.7 tonnes of carbon dioxide equivalent and $3.99 million over a seven year horizon, a saving of 50.2 tonnes and $100,000 compared to built four subloop configuration. The optimum remains on the Pareto front in all 15 scenarios of a one at a time sensitivity sweep. A semi-analytical decision rule generalizes the result, predicting four subloops for Aurora, two for El Capitan, and one for LUMI. When reliability is treated as a hard constraint set by operations policy, the four subloop Frontier deployment is consistent with the constrained optimum.

</details>


### [113] [Minimum settling-time PI control of pure delay processes under a hard non-overshoot constraint: exact boundary-contact characterization and the role of the MID point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15418)
*Senol Gulgonul*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文精确求解了纯延迟过程在零超调约束下的最小调节时间PI控制问题，发现最优增益在特定区间内与延迟带无关，并揭示了最优解与GMID点之间的极限关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PI控制针对纯延迟过程的调节时间优化缺乏精确解，现有方法多依赖近似或数值优化，无法保证零超调硬约束下的最优性。本文旨在建立该问题的精确解析框架。

Method: 将闭环系统建模为中性延迟系统，其阶跃响应在延迟段上为分段多项式，跳变在边界处几何衰减。通过等纹波型接触结构（最大点、跳变点、谷点）刻画约束最优，将接触方程数与增益数匹配，实现精确可解。在闭式区间内直接给出解析解，区间外求解显式多项式方程组。

Result: 在δ∈[4.29%,8.58%]的闭式区间内，最优增益与δ无关：Kp=1-√2/2，KiL=√2/2，调节时间Ts*(δ)=(4-√2-2√δ)L。当δ→0时，最优增益收敛于GMID点（Kp=e^{-2}, KiL=4e^{-2}），但有限δ下δ自适应调优始终优于固定GMID调优（δ=2%时提升约40%）。GMID点是增益的极限而非最优调谐。

Conclusion: 首次精确求解了零超调约束下纯延迟过程的最小调节时间PI控制问题，揭示了最优增益的闭式结构与GMID点的极限关系，并扩展到一阶加延迟过程验证了速度/鲁棒性权衡。

Abstract: We solve, exactly, the problem of minimum settling-time PI control of a pure delay process K e^{-Ls} under the hard time-domain constraint of zero overshoot, y(t) <= 1 for all t. The closed loop is a neutral delay system whose step response is piecewise polynomial on the delay segments, with geometrically decaying jump discontinuities at the segment boundaries t = kL. The constrained optimum is characterized by an equioscillation-type contact structure whose active contacts sit at echo boundaries: kink maxima grazing the setpoint, jumps landing on the settling-band edge, and boundary troughs anchored to it. The number of contact equations equals the number of gains, so the optimum is exactly computable for every band delta. In a closed-form regime, delta in [(3-2 sqrt2)/4, (3-2 sqrt2)/2] approx [4.29%, 8.58%], the optimal gains are independent of delta: K Kp = 1 - sqrt2/2, K Ki L = sqrt2/2, and the optimal settling time is Ts*(delta) = (4 - sqrt2 - 2 sqrt(delta)) L. Outside this window the optimum solves an explicit two-equation polynomial system per regime, and Ts*(delta) is a staircase with exact flats at integer multiples of L from jump-landing pinning. As delta -> 0 the optimal gains converge to K Kp = e^{-2}, K Ki L = 4 e^{-2}, the generic multiplicity-induced-dominancy (GMID) point of the neutral quasipolynomial. The GMID response satisfies the hard constraint and uniquely maximizes the decay rate; yet at every finite delta the delta-adapted optimum strictly beats the fixed GMID tuning, by about 40% at delta = 2%. The MID point is thus the limit of the optimal gains without ever being the optimal tuning. A numerical extension to first-order-plus-time-delay plants quantifies the speed/robustness trade across Ms in [1.39, 1.76].

</details>


### [114] [Positive-Real Identification of Sparse Mori-Hamiltonians from Partial Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15505)
*Mohammad A. Ayoubi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从部分观测数据中直接发现保守系统（哈密顿系统）控制方程的方法，无需重建隐藏状态，证明投影动力学产生的记忆核是正实有理矩阵，其极点对应隐藏固有频率，半正定残差编码耦合关系，可直接从测量信号的互相关中唯一且被动地恢复控制方程和哈密顿量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将部分可观测性视为障碍，需先重建隐藏状态，但该步骤在噪声下不适定且丢弃了物理约束（如能量守恒）。

Method: 证明对于保守（哈密顿）系统，将动力学投影到可测量坐标上会产生一个无损正实有理矩阵形式的内存核，其极点对应隐藏自然频率，半正定残差编码耦合信息；直接从测量信号的自相关函数中读取控制方程和底层哈密顿量，无需神经网络。

Result: 在线性、非线性和混沌系统上，在 realistic 噪声条件下验证了方法的有效性，能够恢复通过构造保持能量守恒的可解释运动方程。

Conclusion: 该方法为力学、流体和等离子体物理等领域提供了一种通用的从部分测量中恢复守恒系统控制方程的工具，具有唯一性和物理被动性保证。

Abstract: Discovering the governing equations of a physical system from data is a central goal across the sciences, yet in most experiments only a few states are accessible while the rest stay hidden. Existing approaches treat this partial observability as an obstacle to be removed by first reconstructing the hidden state -- a step that is ill-posed under noise and that discards the physical constraints, such as energy conservation, that the true dynamics obey. We show that for conservative (Hamiltonian) systems no reconstruction is needed: projecting the dynamics onto the measured coordinates yields a memory kernel that we prove to be a lossless positive-real rational matrix, whose poles are the hidden natural frequencies and whose positive-semidefinite residues encode the couplings. The governing equation -- and the underlying Hamiltonian -- can therefore be read directly from the autocorrelation of the measured signal, with guarantees of uniqueness and physical passivity, and without neural networks. We validate the approach on linear, nonlinear, and chaotic systems under realistic noise. By recovering interpretable equations of motion that conserve energy by construction from partial measurements, the method offers a common tool for problems spanning mechanics, fluid and plasma physics, and beyond.

</details>


### [115] [Nodal Frequency Stability-Constrained UC & ED for Renewable-Dominated Power Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15706)
*Marena Trujillo,David Cole,Bri-Mathias Hodge*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 针对高比例可再生能源电力系统中频率稳定性问题，提出了一种考虑各母线本地频率稳定性约束的机组组合与经济调度优化框架，并通过两种方法（增加火电机组或限制最大出力机组）抑制瞬时频率过低，验证了所提方法优于基于惯量中心（COI）的传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高比例逆变器基资源（IBRs）接入下，系统频率动态快速变化且各母线频率轨迹差异显著，传统基于惯量中心（COI）的频率稳定性约束难以准确反映局部频率问题，亟需一种能够考虑各母线本地频率稳定性约束的优化框架。

Method: 提出了一种内嵌各母线频率稳定性约束的机组组合与经济调度优化框架。针对最大发电机故障导致的瞬时频率过低问题，设计了两种缓解方法：1) 增加火电机组约束；2) 限制当前解中最大出力机组的最大功率输出。

Result: 两种方法均能有效消除导致瞬时频率低于58 Hz的调度方案；第二种方法在维持频率稳定性的同时，使得生产成本的增加幅度最小。

Conclusion: 所提出的考虑各母线频率稳定性约束的优化框架，相比基于COI原理的传统方法，能更有效地实现频率稳定性约束下的机组组合与经济调度。

Abstract: In modern power systems with high shares of renewable, inverter-based resources (IBRs), frequency stability becomes more complex due to the fast dynamics of IBRs and frequency trajectories that vary significantly from bus to bus. In this paper, we present an optimization framework for unit commitment and economic dispatch with endogenous frequency stability constraints at each bus. Two approaches for mitigating excessively low instantaneous frequency values in the event of the largest generator contingency are proposed: 1) by introducing a constraint requiring more thermal generation, and 2) by constraining the maximum power output of the generator that had the largest power output in the incumbent solution. Both approaches proved effective in eliminating dispatch scenarios that resulted in instantaneous frequencies below 58 Hz, while the second approach minimized the difference in production cost values from the non-stability-constrained case. Overall, the results indicate that the proposed optimization framework is a more effective alternative to frequency stability-constrained unit commitment and economic dispatch (UC & ED) than those based on the center-of-inertia (COI) principle.

</details>


### [116] [Age and Stability Trade-offs in Remote Monitoring Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15746)
*Nitya Sathyavageeswaran,Anand D. Sarwate,Roy D. Yates,Narayan Mandayam*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了IoT服务中服务器处理队列作业与生成状态更新之间的权衡，将调度决策建模为MDP，目标是最小化平均信息年龄与平均队列长度的加权和。发现最优调度策略是单调的队列依赖年龄阈值，不同优先级下开关曲线形状不同，并与启发式策略进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT服务中共享服务器需要同时处理作业队列和生成状态更新，这两者之间存在根本性权衡——队列稳定性与数据新鲜度之间的冲突。

Method: 将调度决策建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），以最小化平均AoI和平均队列长度的加权和为目标，推导最优调度策略。

Result: 最优调度策略是单调的队列依赖年龄阈值；不同优先级下开关曲线形状不同。

Conclusion: 最优MDP策略优于启发式策略，为IoT系统中信息新鲜度与队列稳定性的平衡提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Timely information is important in a wide variety of Internet of Things (IoT) services in which a shared server must manage two competing tasks: (i) processing a queue of jobs, and (ii) generating status updates to a remote monitor. This creates a fundamental trade-off between queue stability and data freshness. In this work, we model this scheduling decision as a Markov Decision Process (MDP) with the objective of minimizing a weighted sum of the average Age of Information (AoI) and the average queue length. We show that the optimal scheduling strategy is a queue-dependent age threshold which is monotonic. The shape of the switching curve differs according to different priority regimes. Finally, we compare the optimal MDP policy against heuristic policies.

</details>


### [117] [An Optimal Power Management Policy for Hydrogen-based Hybrid Aero Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15845)
*Faezeh Pak,Uto Perra,Frank Willems,Theo Hofman,Mauro Salazar*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对氢基混合航空发动机（燃气轮机+固体氧化物燃料电池SOFC），提出了一种基于二次准稳态模型和KKT最优性条件的功率管理策略，实现了闭式全局最优稳态功率分配，并在高保真模型上验证了高精度（功率分配误差<1.5%，油耗预测误差<0.7%），最终推导出以SOFC最高允许温度为边界的最小油耗启发式控制律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为氢基混合航空推进系统（燃气轮机+SOFC）设计一种最优功率管理策略，以最小化燃料消耗，同时满足各部件运行约束，并为机载简易实现提供理论基础。

Method: 1. 建立推进系统的二次准稳态模型，将最小燃料最优控制问题建模为燃气轮机与SOFC之间功率分配的函数；2. 利用KKT最优性条件及模型的局部凸性和单调性，推导出闭式全局最优稳态功率分配；3. 在高保真集成静态模型上验证策略，评估功率分配和油耗预测的归一化均方根误差。

Result: 功率分配归一化均方根误差小于1.5%，预测燃料消耗误差小于0.7%；最优策略可转化为启发式控制律：请求不超过SOFC最高工作温度的最大SOFC功率。

Conclusion: 所提出的最优功率管理策略可在高精度下实现最小燃料消耗，并可通过简单的启发式控制律实现，为低计算开销的机载实现铺平了道路。

Abstract: This paper presents a power management policy for a hydrogen-based hybrid aero engine combining a gas turbine and a solid oxide fuel cell (SOFC). Specifically, we first identify a quadratic quasi-steady-state model of the propulsion system and formulate the minimum-fuel optimal control problem as a function of the power split between gas turbine and SOFC that captures the interconnections between the components and accounts for their operational limits. Second, leveraging the Karush-Kuhn-Tucker optimality conditions and partial convexity and monotonicity model properties, we compute the globally optimal steady-state power split for the different phases of the flight in closed form. Finally, we verify this power management policy with a high-fidelity integrated static model %simulator across different flight phases, revealing in less than 1.5 % normalized root mean square error in power allocation and less than 0.7 % in predicted fuel consumption. Our results show that the optimal power management policy can be translated into a heuristic control law requesting the highest SOFC power that does not exceed its maximum operating temperature, ultimately paving the way for minimal-effort on-board implementations.

</details>


### [118] [Fast Convergence and Robustness for Two-Layered Forgetting Recursive Least Square under Finite Excitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15851)
*Satoshi Tsuruhara,Kazuhisa Ito*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双层遗忘的递归最小二乘（TLF-RLS）方法，结合重构指数遗忘（ReEF）算法，解决了非持续激励条件下参数估计的稳定性、鲁棒性和收敛速度之间的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如EF-RLS、DF-RLS）在非持续激励条件下存在稳定性保证不足、对系统变化鲁棒性差、收敛速度慢等固有缺陷，亟需一种能同时保证全局指数稳定性和快速收敛的新方法。

Method: 提出TLF-RLS方法，内层使用方向遗忘（DF）构建增广回归矩阵确保稳定性，外层使用指数遗忘（EF）但会导致估计饱和；进一步提出ReEF算法，通过基于当前协方差矩阵特征值大小的变量和矩阵遗忘机制动态调整遗忘因子，解决外层EF引起的估计饱和问题。

Result: 理论分析表明，在温和假设下TLF-RLS结合ReEF算法能保证条件数的一致最终有界性；数值仿真验证了该方法在非持续激励条件下兼具快速参数收敛和对系统变化的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了非持续激励下参数估计中收敛速度与鲁棒性之间的权衡问题，在暂态和稳态响应中均表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Under nonpersistent excitation (non-PE) conditions, conventional methods such as exponential forgetting (EF) or directional forgetting (DF) recursive least squares (RLS) that rely on direct regressor vectors exhibit inherent limitations in terms of stability guarantees for parameter errors, robustness to system changes, and convergence rates. To address these limitations, this study introduces a novel two-layer forgetting RLS (TLF-RLS) identification method based on an augmented regressor matrix constructed using DF, which ensures global exponential stability and enhances robustness under non-PE condition. However, the convergence rate of the parameter is strongly dependent on the forgetting factor because of the introduction of EF in the outer layer, which causes an estimation windup under non-PE condition. To address this issue, a novel reconfiguration-based EF (ReEF) algorithm is proposed, which is achieved through variable- and matrix-based forgetting related to the magnitude of the eigenvalues of the current covariance matrix. Theoretical analysis indicates that TLF-RLS with ReEF algorithm guarantees uniform ultimate boundedness of the condition number under mild assumptions. Consequently, the proposed method resolves the trade-off between fast parameter convergence and robustness in both transient and steady-state responses under changes in system characteristics. Numerical simulations of three aforementioned cases demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [119] [Stability Analysis in Multi-Constraint Safety Filters for Linear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15942)
*Shima Sadat Mousavi,Pol Mestres,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出几何框架分析多约束安全滤波器（基于控制障碍函数）的稳定性，通过显式活动集实现、最小相位检验和LMI条件，区分发散与有界收敛行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多约束安全滤波器虽保证前向不变性，但可能改变标称稳定性，且不稳定模式何时导致发散或收敛尚不明确。

Method: 建立几何框架：利用显式活动集实现，证明非空活动集平衡点位于对应约束面上，不稳定方向因指数约束强制执行而与该面相切；通过最小相位检验表征模式稳定性，利用衰退锥验证固定活动集下的发散性，基于李雅普诺夫和拉萨尔不变原理推导LMI条件。

Result: 给出了全局指数稳定或系统有界的可处理LMI条件；通过最小相位检验区分稳定与不稳定模式；利用衰退锥方法判定发散。

Conclusion: 系统性地解决了线性系统仿射状态约束下多约束安全滤波器的稳定性分析问题，为实际应用中判断系统行为提供了可计算判据。

Abstract: Multi-constraint safety filters based on control barrier functions for linear systems with affine state constraints yield continuous piecewise-affine closed-loop dynamics and may introduce boundary equilibria and unstable active-set modes. Although they guarantee forward invariance, they can change nominal stability, and it remains unclear when unstable modes cause divergence versus bounded, convergent behavior. This paper develops a geometric framework to separate these cases: leveraging explicit active-set realizations, we show that equilibria associated with nonempty active sets lie on the corresponding constraint faces and that any unstable directions are tangent to those faces due to exponential enforcement of the active constraints. We characterize mode stability via a minimum-phase test, certify divergence under fixed active sets using recession cones, and derive tractable linear-matrix-inequality conditions for global exponential stability or boundedness using Lyapunov and LaSalle arguments.

</details>


### [120] [Artificial Intelligence for Power-Converter-Rich Electrical Systems: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15948)
*Pengfeng Lin,Yuan Gao,Yuxi Tang,Muhammad Waqas Qaisar,Peifeng Hui,Chuanlin Zhang,Miao Zhu,Peng Wang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文综述了人工智能在富含电力变换器的电气系统（如可再生能源、电气化交通和逆变器基资源）全生命周期中的应用，涵盖设计、实时控制、系统级运行和合规治理四个阶段，重点分析了部署关键缺口（如稳定性认证、约束满足、可解释性、数据效率、安全等）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 富含电力变换器的电气系统具有强非线性动力学、多物理设计权衡、快速控制需求以及日益增长的可信性和网络安全约束，传统仅依赖物理建模、顺序优化和规则驱动的流程已难以应对这些挑战，亟需引入AI方法。

Method: 采用生命周期与部署成熟度视角，对现有文献进行系统分类：设计阶段（代理建模、拓扑与参数综合、EMI/EMC感知优化、可靠性设计、知识辅助流程）；控制阶段（监督学习、强化学习、学习增强预测控制、安全约束学习）；运行阶段（微电网协调、预测、配电网可观性、隐私保护协调、网络弹性运行）。

Result: 构建了一个分类体系，明确了AI在电力变换器系统中作为工程支持工具已有应用价值的领域，以及在自主或安全关键部署前仍需进一步验证的环节（稳定性认证、约束满足、可解释性、外推能力、数据效率、仿真到现实迁移、嵌入式延迟、网络安全、隐私及标准对齐）。

Conclusion: AI在富含电力变换器的电气系统中已展现出作为工程支持工具的价值，但在自主或安全关键场景下部署前，仍需在稳定性认证、约束满足、可解释性、数据效率、仿真到现实迁移、延迟、网络安全、隐私和标准对齐等方面进行更充分的验证。

Abstract: Power-converter-rich electrical systems, formed by renewable generation, electrified transportation, and inverter-based resources, exhibit strongly nonlinear dynamics, multi-physics design tradeoffs, fast control requirements, and growing reliability and cybersecurity constraints. These characteristics strain workflows that rely only on physics-based modeling, sequential optimization, and rule-based operation. This paper reviews artificial intelligence (AI) for power-converter-rich electrical systems through a life-cycle and deployment-readiness perspective. The literature is organized across converter design, real-time control, system-level operation, and compliance-oriented governance. For design, we examine surrogate modeling, topology and parameter synthesis, EMI/EMC-aware optimization, reliability-oriented design, and knowledge-assisted workflows. For control, we compare supervised learning, reinforcement learning, learning-augmented predictive control, and safety-constrained learning according to their role in closed-loop implementation. For operations, we focus on microgrid coordination, forecasting, distribution-system observability, privacy-preserving coordination, and cyber-resilient operation where converter-interfaced resources shape the operating problem. Across these stages, the review emphasizes deployment-critical gaps, including stability certification, constraint satisfaction, interpretability, extrapolation, data efficiency, sim-to-real transfer, embedded latency, cybersecurity, privacy, and standards alignment. The resulting taxonomy is intended to clarify where AI is already useful as an engineering support tool and where further validation is needed before autonomous or safety-critical deployment.

</details>


### [121] [SparseCol: A 1320 BTOPS/W Precision-scalable NPU Exploiting Training-free Structured Bit-level Sparsity and Dynamic Dataflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16016)
*Man Shi,Vikram Jain,Weijie Jiang,Chao Fang,Antony Joseph,Wim Dehaene,Marian Verhelst*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: SparseCol是一款基于比特列稀疏性（Bit-Column Sparsity）的动态数据流处理器，在16nm CMOS工艺下实现1320 BTOPS/W峰值效率，相比现有稀疏处理器效率提升6.8倍，广泛应用于CNN、RNN和Transformer等AI任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有比特串行处理器在利用比特级稀疏性时面临两难困境：要么因非零比特不规则模式导致低内存访问和计算效率，要么因复杂的在线调度机制引入大量面积开销。本文旨在解决这一权衡问题。

Method: 提出SparseCol处理器，利用结构化比特级稀疏性（比特列稀疏性，无需重训练），并采用动态数据流架构解决现有比特串行方案中的硬件利用率不足问题。

Result: 在16nm CMOS工艺下实现1320 BTOPS/W峰值效率，比现有稀疏处理器效率提升6.8倍；CNN分类任务系统级效率745.02 BTOPS/W，Transformer架构系统级效率850.5 BTOPS/W。

Conclusion: SparseCol通过比特列稀疏性和动态数据流架构，在不牺牲精度的情况下显著提升了比特级稀疏处理器的硬件利用率和能效，适用于多种AI应用场景。

Abstract: Bit-serial computation enables sequential processing of data at the bit level, providing several advantages, such as scalable computational precision. This approach has gained significant attention, especially for exploiting bit-level sparsity in AI workloads. While current bit-serial processors leverage bit-level sparsity to eliminate the computation associated with zero bits, they face a fundamental trade-off: either they suffer from low memory-access and computation efficiency caused by irregular patterns of non-zero bits, or they incur substantial area overhead from complex online scheduling mechanisms required to reorganize bit-level data and preserve memory access and computation regularity. Therefore, we present the SparseCol processor, designed to harness extensive bit sparsity while maintaining high hardware utilization across various AI applications, including CNNs, RNNs, and transformers. In contrast to traditional methods, SparseCol exploits structured bit-level sparsity, denoted by bit-column sparsity, without requiring any re-training. Furthermore, SparseCol implements a dynamic dataflow architecture that tackles hardware under-utilization issues commonly found in existing bit-serial solutions. Fabricated in 16nm CMOS node, SparseCol delivers 1320 BTOPS/W (BTOPS represents Binary Tera-Operations Per Second, calculated as #W bits x #A bits TOPS) peak efficiency while maintaining accuracy, outperforming SotA sparse processors in terms of efficiency by 6.8x. Comprehensive evaluations on CNN classification tasks and transformer architectures demonstrate system-level efficiencies of 745.02 BTOPS/W and 850.5 BTOPS/W, respectively.

</details>


### [122] [Anisotropic Template Ansätze for Robust Positive Invariance under State-Dependent Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16068)
*Abdelrahman Ramadan,Melissa Greeff,Sidney Givigi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于高斯过程（GP）导出的正定矩阵场来构建各向异性扰动协方差下的鲁棒正不变集方法，通过将固定椭球模板经矩阵场映射实现自适应管状不变集，在线计算仅需一次GP协方差查询和小矩阵平方根，无需在线迭代或LMI求解。四旋翼仿真显示3D速度管体积减少195倍，7D联合空间减少2.1×10^5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒不变集方法通常假设各向同性或固定协方差的扰动，无法有效处理状态和输入依赖的、具有各向异性协方差结构的扰动，导致保守性过高或计算负担重。

Method: 1) 将固定椭球模板通过GP导出的正定矩阵场进行映射，生成各向异性自适应管状不变集；2) 推导学习场与Schur稳定动力学耦合的LMI条件；3) 提出各向同性退化的回退方案（膨胀因子r=1/(1-γ_cl)）；4) 学习周期内冻结矩阵场，在线仅需一次GP协方差查询和矩阵平方根运算。

Result: 四旋翼仿真：3D速度管体积减少195倍；7D速度-控制联合子空间减少2.1×10^5倍；相比非自适应同伦基线方法显著降低保守性。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够在各向异性扰动下实现显著更紧致的鲁棒不变集，且在线计算极高效（无迭代、无LMI求解），适用于实时控制场景。扩展版本补充了完整证明、离线/在线复杂度分析以及控制器扫描、收缩和投影面积研究。

Abstract: We establish sufficient conditions for robust positive invariance under state- and input-dependent disturbances with anisotropic covariance structure. The proposed ansatz maps a fixed ellipsoidal template through a GP-derived positive-definite matrix field, subsuming scalar homothetic scaling while retaining finite graph-based verification. The resulting LMI conditions couple the learned field to Schur-stable dynamics; an isotropic fallback with inflation factor $r=1/(1-γ_{\mathrm{cl}})$ proves admissibility. During each learning epoch the field is frozen, so online tube evaluation is one GP covariance query and a small matrix square root, with no online set iteration or LMI solve. Quadrotor simulations show a $195\times$ reduction in 3D velocity-tube volume and a $2.1{\times}10^5$ reduction in the joint 7D velocity-control subspace relative to a non-adaptive homothetic baseline. This extended version adds full proofs, a separated offline/online complexity analysis, and controller-sweep, contraction, and projection-area studies.

</details>


### [123] [Distributed Safe Consensus Under Asymmetric Input and Time-Varying Output Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16116)
*Abhinav Sinha,Shashi Ranjan Kumar*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对单积分多智能体系统，在无向连通图上研究同时具有非对称执行器约束和输出安全约束的分布式安全一致性问题。通过引入势垒坐标变换和双層控制器结构，实现了输入可容许性、输出安全集前向不变性和渐进同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式一致性研究大多假设执行器无约束或仅考虑对称约束，且输出安全约束与执行器约束往往被分开处理。实际系统中执行器存在非对称饱和特性，同时系统输出需保持在安全区间内，亟需统一的控制框架同时处理这两类约束。

Method: 1) 引入连续可微非对称执行器动力学模型；2) 基于时变安全区间设计势垒坐标变换；3) 提出双层控制器：图基协调层+执行器侧跟踪层；4) 在变换坐标下设计分布式同步律。

Result: 闭环解完整，所有信号有界；执行器输入严格保持在非对称界内；智能体输出始终在安全区间内；变换后的同步误差指数收敛；原始输出渐进同步到设计者选定的嵌入公共安全区间的可容许轨迹。

Conclusion: 提出的框架能同时处理非对称执行器约束和时变输出安全约束，实现安全分布式一致性，数值仿真验证了有效性。

Abstract: This paper studies safe distributed consensus for single-integrator multi-agent systems over connected undirected graphs under simultaneous asymmetric actuator constraints and output safety constraints. Each agent is equipped with a continuously differentiable asymmetric actuator dynamics that maps a commanded control signal to the realized plant input while keeping the latter strictly inside a prescribed admissible interval. To address output safety, a barrier-coordinate transformation is introduced over a common time-varying safe interval, and a distributed synchronization law is designed in the transformed coordinates. The resulting controller integrates a graph-based coordination layer with an actuator-side tracking layer, thereby enabling simultaneous enforcement of input admissibility, forward invariance of the safe output set, and asymptotic synchronization. For compact admissible sets of initial conditions, it is shown that the closed-loop solution is complete, all signals remain bounded, the actuator inputs remain strictly within their asymmetric bounds, and the agent outputs remain inside the prescribed safe interval for all time. Moreover, the transformed synchronization errors converge exponentially to zero, and the original agent outputs asymptotically synchronize to a designer-selected admissible trajectory embedded in the common safe interval. Numerical simulations validate the proposed framework and demonstrate safe consensus under both asymmetric actuation bounds and time-varying output constraints.

</details>


### [124] [Geospatial sensitivity of transmission-constrained ACOPF to generator retirement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16117)
*Willa Gutowski,Ulyana Shyrokaya,Nicholson Koukpaizan,Joshua Hambrick,Slaven Peles,Eve Tsybina*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于HPC的框架，通过k近邻模型和电压等级调整的邻居模型，快速检测发电机退役对输电受限电网中电压幅值和角度的影响，并在70,000节点合成电网上验证了8,000多个退役场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国面临资源充足性挑战：新增负荷规模空前，而老旧发电资产正在退役。在输电受限电网中，难以确定哪些机组可以安全退役，哪些需要延长寿命直至新发电资源建成。传统分析耗时巨大，因为输电受限的ACOPF计算密集，全面比较和优先级排序可能需要数百或数千个场景。

Method: 基于HPC的框架，利用简单的k近邻模型和电压等级调整的邻居模型，计算并地理空间映射发电机退役对稳态电压幅值和角度的影响。

Result: 在70,000节点输电受限合成电网上，对超过8,000个发电机退役场景进行了验证。

Conclusion: 该HPC框架能够高效检测发电机退役的影响，克服了传统ACOPF计算耗时的瓶颈，为输电受限电网中发电机退役决策提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The US faces a growing resource adequacy challenge: new loads are being added at unprecedented scale while aging generating assets are being retired. In transmission-constrained grids, it is difficult to determine which units can be safely retired and which cannot be retired and instead require lifetime extensions until new generation can be built. Historically, this analysis was prohibitively time consuming. Transmission-constrained AC optimal power flow (ACOPF) is computationally intensive, and a thorough comparison and prioritization of generators could require hundreds or thousands of scenarios. We present an HPC-enabled framework that enables computation and geospatial mapping of the effects of generator retirement in terms of voltage magnitude and angle effects in the steady state. Specifically, our framework detects the effects of generator retirement using a simple k-nearest-neighbors model and a voltage-class-adjusted neighbor model. We demonstrate the results on over 8,000 generator retirement scenarios for a 70,000-bus transmission-constrained synthetic grid.

</details>


### [125] [Data-driven Control with Real-time Uncertainty Compensation for Multi-Fuel Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16171)
*Rajasree Sarkar,Arunava Banerjee,Sathya Aswath Govind Raju,Ishan Berk Altiner,Zongxuan Sun,Kenneth Kim,Chol-Bum Mike Keown*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于高斯过程回归（GPR）和数据驱动的实时不确定性补偿框架，用于多燃料压缩点火（CI）发动机的燃烧相位控制。通过引入伪发动机转速概念，结合GPR模型逆推与不确定性补偿器，实现了有限燃烧周期内控制输入的实时自适应调整，并给出了有限时间收敛的理论保证。仿真结果验证了该方法在多种工况下的有效性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多燃料CI发动机在宽工况下实现一致且最优的燃烧相位面临重大挑战，尤其是存在模型不确定性的情况下。现有方法难以兼顾燃料灵活性和实时自适应调节能力，亟需一种能够实时补偿不确定性、鲁棒且可扩展的控制框架。

Method: 1）利用高斯过程回归（GPR）对多燃料CI发动机的非线性、燃料依赖的燃烧过程进行建模；2）通过GPR模型逆推合成标称控制输入；3）引入不确定性补偿器，基于伪发动机转速概念动态调整控制输入，应对工况变化和模型误差；4）理论证明控制器的有限时间收敛性。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的方法能够在实时条件下将燃烧相位调节至期望值，有效应对工况动态变化和模型不确定性，实现有限燃烧周期内的收敛。

Conclusion: 该数据驱动的实时不确定性补偿框架为多燃料CI发动机提供了一种可扩展、自适应的燃烧控制解决方案，兼具理论收敛保证和实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Multi-fuel compression ignition (CI) engines offer superior power density and fuel flexibility. However, achieving consistent and optimal combustion phasing across a wide range of operating conditions remains a major challenge, particularly in the presence of modeling uncertainties. This paper presents a novel, data-driven real-time uncertainty compensation framework for combustion control in multi-fuel CI engines. The proposed approach introduces a pseudo-engine speed that enables dynamic adaptation of control inputs in response to uncertainty affecting the engine. To model the underlying combustion process, a Gaussian Process Regression (GPR) model is first trained on available input-output data, capturing the nonlinear and fuel-dependent behavior across varying operating conditions. Control inputs are then synthesized through model inversion of the learned GPR surrogate and augmented with an uncertainty compensator designed to mitigate deviations caused by dynamic variations in operating conditions and model inaccuracies. This integrated control strategy allows for real-time input corrections within a finite number of combustion cycles. Theoretical analysis establishes finite-time convergence guarantees for the proposed controller. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method steers the combustion phasing to the desired value in real-time, providing a scalable and adaptive control solution for multi-fuel CI engine operation.

</details>


### [126] [Geometry-Driven Islanding Detection and Fault Classification for Grid-Forming Inverters: A Normally Hyperbolic Invariant Manifold Framework with Physics-Derived Thresholds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16282)
*Shubh Arekar,Aditi Ramteke,Kshitij Gaikwad,Tejal Jadhav,Shashank Verma,Madhavi Parimi,Sushama Wagh*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于几何理论（NAIM）与随机假设检验的构网型逆变器故障检测与分类框架，通过将GFM下垂流形识别为NAIM，利用Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程建模噪声，构造检测统计量D_t实现无阈值调参的精确检测，并在8000次蒙特卡罗仿真中验证了理论结果，同时实现了孤岛与电压故障的分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GFM逆变器的故障检测方法缺乏严格的几何理论基础，且存在阈值调参依赖、误报率控制不精确等问题，需要一种无调参、渐近精确的检测与分类框架。

Method: 1) 将GFM下垂流形M_0识别为NAIM；2) 将电网噪声下的横向波动建模为Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程，通过代数Lyapunov方程求解长程协方差；3) 构造检测统计量D_t，零假设下收敛于χ²(2)分布，得到无调参阈值D_α=-2lnα；4) 通过模态分解分离频率与电压贡献，实现故障分类。

Result: 纠正了先前公式中2倍的错误；8000次MC仿真验证了九个窗口长度和三个显著性水平下的性能；Berry-Esseen界d_KS≤1.6704/(βT_w)得到经验确认；最小窗口条件T_w≥10/β_min≈1.0 s满足IEEE 1547-2018的2秒检测要求；协同设计定理揭示增大(ω_f,ω_v)可同时扩大Fenichel谱隙、收紧零协方差、降低误报率；案例验证了正常工况接受、软孤岛快速检测及10%电压暂降的准确识别。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了无调参、渐近精确的GFM逆变器故障检测与分类，理论完备性强，计算效率高，满足IEEE标准要求，并具备故障分类能力而无需额外传感器。

Abstract: This paper presents a geometry-driven detection and fault-classification framework for grid-forming (GFM) inverters based on normally hyperbolic invariant manifolds (NAIM) and stochastic hypothesis testing. The GFM droop manifold $\mathcal{M}_0$ is identified as a NAIM of the closed-loop dynamics. Transverse fluctuations under grid noise are modeled as an Ornstein--Uhlenbeck process, and the long-run covariance is obtained from the algebraic Lyapunov equation. The detection statistic $D_t=T_w\barξ_{\perp}^{\top}Σ_{\mathrm{long}}^{-1}\barξ_{\perp}$ converges to $χ^2(2)$ under the null hypothesis, yielding the tuning-free threshold $D_α=-2\lnα$ and an asymptotically exact false-alarm rate $α$. A factor-of-2 error in earlier formulations is corrected and validated using 8,000 Monte Carlo realizations over nine window lengths and three significance levels. The Berry--Esseen bound $d_{\mathrm{KS}}\leq1.6704/(βT_w)$ is confirmed empirically. The minimum window condition $T_w\geq10/β_{\min}\approx1.0$ s, where $β_{\min}=\min(ω_f,ω_v)$, satisfies the IEEE 1547-2018 two-second detection requirement. A co-design theorem shows that increasing $(ω_f,ω_v)$ simultaneously enlarges the Fenichel spectral gap, tightens the null covariance, and reduces the false-alarm rate. Modal decomposition separates frequency and voltage contributions, enabling classification of islanding and voltage faults without additional sensors. Case studies confirm correct acceptance of normal operation, rapid detection of soft islanding, and accurate identification of a 10\% voltage sag.

</details>


### [127] [An Adjoint-based Neural Regulator for Real-Time Optimal Control with State Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16303)
*Isaiah A. Agboola,Yuxin Tong,Uduak Inyang-Udoh*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于学习的控制框架（ANR），通过学习神经共态（伴随）策略编码最优性，并在运行时通过凸投影强制执行约束，在非线性约束控制任务中达到与非线性模型预测控制相当的性能，但计算成本降低两个数量级以上，且在未见场景中表现优于强化学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非线性最优控制方法（如NMPC）计算成本高，难以实时应用；而强化学习方法在训练分布外场景泛化能力差。需要一种既能保证实时性、又能提供最优性和安全保证的控制框架。

Method: 基于Pontryagin最小值原理，学习一个神经伴随策略（共态网络）来编码系统哈密顿量中的最优性结构；运行时通过高效凸投影单独强制执行可行性约束（执行器限制和安全约束以控制屏障函数形式表达）。

Result: 在非线性约束控制任务（unicycle模型）上，ANR达到与NMPC相当的优化性能，计算成本降低两个数量级以上；在未见场景中性能近乎不变，显著优于强化学习方法。

Conclusion: ANR框架有效结合了最优控制理论的结构性保证与深度学习的计算效率，为实时安全关键控制提供了一种有前景的替代方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces a learning-based control framework for real-time constrained optimal control of nonlinear systems with safety guarantees based on the Pontryagin's Minimum Principle. The approach learns a neural co-state (adjoint) policy that encodes optimality through the system Hamiltonian, rather than directly approximating a control law. Feasibility is enforced separately at runtime through an efficient convex projection that incorporates actuator limits and safety constraints expressed as control barrier functions. We refer to this framework as an adjoint-based neural regulator (ANR) as it yields a controller that satisfies constraints while retaining the optimality structure encoded by the learned adjoint. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework on nonlinear constrained control tasks using a unicycle model. The ANR achieves performance at par with nonlinear model predictive control at more than two orders of magnitude lower computational cost, while exhibiting near-invariant performance across unseen scenarios, thus, significantly outperforming reinforcement learning methods in out-of-training-distribution regimes.

</details>


### [128] [Sustainable Heating with Karma: A Simulation Study of the KTH Live-In Lab](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16321)
*Mahsa Farjadnia,Ezzat Elokda,Angela Fontan,Marco Molinari*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种非货币的“业力经济”机制，通过模拟研究验证其在KTH Live-In Lab数字模型中促进可持续供暖行为的公平性和有效性，为后续人类在环实验提供设计指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑供暖占全球CO2足迹的10%，现有基于货币的需求响应政策歧视低收入家庭，且对不主动参与实时控制信号的 occupants 存在财务风险。需要一种非货币的公平机制来促进可持续供暖行为。

Method: 在KTH Live-In Lab的数字模型上进行仿真研究，设计并调优基于消费记忆的业力经济机制，将其与传统无记忆供暖操作进行对比，分析其对窗户开启行为的影响。

Result: 提供了业力经济在供暖分配中的预期效果初始估计，验证了其在公平性和效率方面的潜力，并展示了引入消费记忆（业力）对窗户开启行为的影响。

Conclusion: 业力经济作为一种非货币的需求响应机制，能够公平且高效地促进可持续供暖行为，值得进一步通过人类在环实验进行验证。

Abstract: Space heating in buildings accounts for 10% of the global CO2 footprint. The widespread adoption of energy-efficient heating technology, e.g., heat pumps, could help reduce this figure, but technology alone may not suffice to reach carbon neutrality. Additionally, human occupants have an important role to play by adopting sustainable heating behaviors, e.g., avoid excessive window opening in the winter or (pre-)heat their units while clean energy is abundant. Thus far demand response policies aimed at promoting these behaviors have been monetary, which discriminates against low-income households and exposes human occupants who do not actively engage with real-time control signals to financial risks. This paper instead investigates the suitability of a non-monetary karma economy for promoting sustainable heating behaviors. Karma leverages the repeated and dynamic nature of heating energy allocations to attain climate targets both fairly and efficiently over time without resorting to financial means. As a first step towards experimentally validating the karma concept with real human occupants in the KTH Live-In Lab, we perform a simulation study on a digital model of the Live-In Lab. The study provides initial estimates of expected effects to guide the design of human-in-the-loop experiments, as well as assists with designing and tuning the karma economy in this context. As a specific example, we investigate how incorporating consumption memory in the form of karma affects window opening behaviors in comparison to conventional memory-less heating operation.

</details>


### [129] [Transient-Safe Platooning via Dynamic Headway](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16374)
*Gal Barkai,J Ér Émie Kreiss,Vineeth S Varma,Irinel-Constantin Morarescu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对自动驾驶汽车队列的计算高效控制框架，在保证严格瞬态安全性的同时实现队列稳定，通过引入一种自然收敛于恒定时间间隔（CTH）的间距策略，将队列安全建立为归纳性质，并推导了非线性饱和控制律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶汽车队列管理需要在队列稳定性与严格安全性之间取得平衡，尤其在激进的瞬态工况（如高速公路合流）下挑战加剧。CTH间距策略作为行业标准，在速度显著偏差时缺乏形式化安全保证。

Method: 采用线性时不变模型描述每辆车的动力学；引入一种在稳态下自然收敛到CTH的间距策略；将队列安全建立为归纳性质；推导了领头跟随者的非线性饱和控制律；给出了保证速度非负性和队列安全的充分初始条件。

Result: 数值算例表明所提出的方法即使在非标称设置下也可能适用。

Conclusion: 该方法对任何基于CTH的跟随者控制律都能保证整个队列的速度非负性和安全性，为自动驾驶队列在瞬态工况下的形式化安全提供了计算高效的控制框架。

Abstract: Managing autonomous vehicle platoons requires a delicate balance between string stability and rigorous safety. This challenge is intensified by aggressive transients, such as highway merging. Although Constant Time Headway (CTH) spacing is the industry standard for Cooperative Adaptive Cruise Control, it lacks formal safety guarantees during significant velocity deviations. This letter proposes a computationally efficient control framework that considers a linear time-invariant model for the dynamics of each vehicle, while ensuring formal transient safety and stability. By introducing a spacing policy that naturally converges to CTH at steady state, we establish platoon safety as an inductive property. We derive a non-linear and saturated control law for the lead follower and provide sufficient initial conditions to guarantee velocity non-negativity and safety throughout the platoon for any CTH-based followers' control law. Numerical examples indicate the proposed methodology may be applicable even under non-nominal setups.

</details>


### [130] [GreenBox: Prototyping of an Automatic Road Accident Detection System with Real-Time Notification SMS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16468)
*Júlio Rocha,Salviano Soares,António Valente,Filipe Cabral Pinto*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出名为"GreenBox"的物联网原型系统，基于Arduino Uno和SIM7600G-H 4G GSM模块，利用冲击按钮、水位传感器和陀螺仪/加速度计检测车辆事故（碰撞、浸水、侧翻），通过SMS向预设联系人发送警报及GPS定位信息，并支持远程熄火和误报取消功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对道路交通事故发生后，受害者可能无法及时联系紧急救援的问题，开发一个自动检测事故并通知预设联系人的物联网系统，以缩短救援响应时间。

Method: 系统硬件包括：四个10kΩ按钮作为碰撞传感器（分别置于车辆四侧）、两个水位传感器（引擎舱和后备箱）、用于检测70°倾角侧翻的陀螺仪/加速度计（通过反正切函数计算）、Arduino Uno微控制器、SIM7600G-H 4G GSM/GPS模块。事故时系统通过继电器切断发动机，并通过AT指令经GSM发送含GPS位置的SMS。支持车主通过SMS远程熄火（防盗）或取消误报。

Result: 成功开发了能够检测碰撞、浸水和侧翻（70°倾角）事故的原型系统，可自动发送含车辆GPS位置信息的SMS警报，并具备远程熄火和误报取消功能。

Conclusion: 该物联网原型系统提供了一种低成本、自动化的交通事故检测与警报方案，能够有效缩短事故后的救援通知时间，并具备防盗和误报处理等实用功能。

Abstract: The Internet of Things (IoT) project, called "GreenBox", proposes the development of a prototype for the detection of road accidents, using sensors and actuators connected to an Arduino Uno and a Global System for Mobile Communications (GSM) card with 4G support, SIM7600G-H (global version) from DF-Robot. This system sends Short Message Service (SMS) to pre-established contacts, alerting, for example, family or friends, so that they immediately contact emergency entities in the event of an accident and provide them with all the necessary information, such as the location of the vehicle. The sensors include four push-buttons, with a resistance of 10kΩ, in order to define their default logical state, representing impact or impact sensors for each side of the vehicle; two water level sensors for the engine compartment and trunk; and a gyroscope/accelerometer to detectrollover from a 70 degrees inclination. The prototype also has a relay that is activated to turn off the engine in the event of a detected accident, preventing further damage. The GSM board has a Global Positioning System (GPS) antenna attached, allowing it to locate the vehicle and determine its speed, moments before the possible accident. The system also allows you to turn off the vehicle via SMS in case of theft. The entire project is prepared for the owner, for example the driver of the vehicle, to cancel all automation, via SMS, in case of false accident detection. Rollover detection is calculated using the arctangent of the accelerometer values and instructions for sending notifications are carried out by AT (Attention Commands) commands, between the microcontroller and the SIM7600 shield.

</details>


### [131] [Can Optimal Dispatch Models Recreate Reality? A Retrospective Analysis of Europe's 2022 Energy Crisis Using PyPSA-Eur](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16486)
*Lukas Karkossa,Marco Saretta,Frederik Erhard Gullach,Marta Victoria*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文使用PyPSA-Eur最优调度模型对2020-2024年欧洲35国的电力市场进行逐时后报，发现高分辨率燃料和CO₂价格时间序列与有限预见期（两周滚动优化）相结合，可显著提升模拟精度，平均SMAPE为20.76%，在燃料价格高波动时期改进最为明显；同时模型倾向于使用更多煤电替代天然气，偏差源于未考虑实际政策干预、调度约束和机组停运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新冠疫情和俄乌战争导致欧洲电力市场出现极端波动，不确定最优调度模型能否在危机条件下可靠复现历史逐时电价。

Method: 基于PyPSA-Eur模型，对35个国家2020-2024年进行逐时后报，比较静态与动态燃料/CO₂价格假设、完美预见期与两周滚动优化（有限预见期）下的节点边际电价与历史ENTSO-E电价，使用SMAPE评估精度。

Result: 高分辨率价格时间序列与有限预见期组合最优，全欧日均负荷加权平均SMAPE为20.76%；改进在燃料价格高波动时期最显著；模型模拟的发电组合中天然气用量显著低于历史、煤电用量更高。

Conclusion: 最优调度模型在危机期间可较好复现电价，但偏差源于未建模的政策干预、调度约束和机组停运等因素。

Abstract: Electricity prices result from the complex interplay of supply and demand, which depends on variable renewable energy production, fuel costs, CO$_2$ price, and grid bottlenecks.Between 2020 and 2024, COVID-19 shifted demand and disrupted supply chains and operations in Europe, while Russia's invasion of Ukraine constrained gas supply, causing exceptional volatility in gas prices. It remains unclear whether optimal dispatch models can reliably replicate historical hourly prices during crises, given the rapid fluctuations in fuel prices and operators' limited foresight.
  In this work, we ask whether an optimal dispatch model, parametrised with historical data on demand, fuel, and CO$_2$ prices, can reproduce the observed market outcomes during this period. We perform hourly hindcasts of electricity generation in 35 countries from 2020 to 2024 using PyPSA-Eur and compare the nodal marginal electricity prices with historical ENTSO-E market prices using the Symmetric Mean Absolute Percentage Error (SMAPE). The scenarios compare static vs dynamic assumptions on fuel and CO$_2$ prices, as well as perfect foresight vs a two-week rolling-horizon optimization.
  Combining high-resolution fuel and CO$_2$ price time series with limited-foresight substantially improves hindcast accuracy, yielding an average SMAPE of 20.76% based on the daily load-weighted average price for the entire Europe. While improvements relative to the scenario with perfect foresight and static price inputs occur, they are most pronounced during periods of high fuel-price volatility, when marginal-cost swings propagate to electricity prices. In 2022, the optimal generation mix in most countries shows substantially less natural gas and more coal than historically observed. Other discrepancies can be attributed to the model's omission of real-world policy interventions, other dispatch constraints, and generator outages.

</details>


### [132] [On the Lyapunov equation with the state matrix in companion form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16492)
*Augusto Ferrante*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 研究Hurwitz伴侣矩阵下连续时间Lyapunov方程解$X$的逐元素非负性，证明当伴侣矩阵仅有实特征值时$X$逐元素非负，并讨论了全非负性在附加符号条件下的恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受正系统理论的启发，探究Lyapunov方程的解$X$是否具有逐元素非负性，而不仅仅是标准理论保证的正半定性。

Method: 将解$X$的每个条目转化为与Cauchy类矩阵相关联的二次型，这些矩阵的条目由初等对称多项式表达，利用这些Cauchy类矩阵的正半定性证明非负性。

Result: 当伴侣矩阵仅有实特征值时，$X$逐元素非负；全非负性一般不成立，但在$A^\top$的特征基下外力向量展开满足附加符号条件时可恢复。

Conclusion: 揭示了Hurwitz伴侣矩阵结构下Lyapunov方程解的非负性特征，为正系统分析提供了新的理论支撑。

Abstract: We study the continuous-time Lyapunov equation under the assumption that the state matrix is a Hurwitz companion matrix. The standard Lyapunov theory implies that the unique solution $X$ is positive semidefinite. Motivated by positive systems, we investigate the question of whether $X$ is entrywise nonnegative. We prove that this is the case when the companion matrix has only real eigenvalues. The proof reduces each entry of $X$ to a quadratic form associated with a class of Cauchy-like matrices whose entries are expressed in terms of elementary symmetric polynomials. The required nonnegativity then follows from the positive semidefiniteness of these Cauchy-like matrices. We also discuss a stronger total-positivity property: total nonnegativity does not hold in general, but it is recovered under an additional sign condition on the expansion of the forcing vector in the eigenbasis of $A^\top$.

</details>


### [133] [Exponential Weighting Model Predictive Control with Observer for Modular Multilevel Converters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16631)
*Sunny Singh,Saurabh Mishra,Dušan M Stipanović,Aleksandra Lekić*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对模块化多电平变换器（MMC），提出了一种采用指数代价函数的模型预测控制（MPC）方案，并结合观测器以提高变换器的动态性能。研究重点解决了预测步长增大时数值病态恶化的问题，通过加权代价函数克服大N_P的限制，分析了约束满足性、控制变量影响、观测器对输出预测的改善以及预设性能对闭环稳定性的先验保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升模块化多电平变换器（MMC）的动态性能，并解决传统MPC在预测步长增大时数值病态恶化的问题。

Method: 提出一种采用指数代价函数的MPC方案，并结合观测器；通过加权代价函数克服大预测步长带来的数值病态问题；分析约束对控制的影响；利用预设性能理论提供闭环稳定性先验保证。

Result: 设计的MPC严格遵循约束条件；控制变量有效影响MMC响应；观测器改善了输出预测，尤其在参考信号设定值变化时；预设性能为闭环稳定性提供了先验保证。

Conclusion: 所提出的指数代价函数MPC结合观测器方案能够有效增强MMC的动态性能，在大预测步长下仍保持良好的数值特性，并具有严格的约束满足性和闭环稳定性保证。

Abstract: In this article, we propose a model predictive control (MPC) scheme with an exponential cost function, along with an observer for the Modular Multilevel Converter (MMC), to enhance converter dynamic performance. In particular, as the prediction horizon $(N_P)$ increases, the numerical conditioning deteriorates rapidly, especially when a large $N_P$ is employed. This research work uses an appropriate cost function weighted to overcome the limitations of a large $N_P$. We further analyse the effects of constraints, observing that the designed MPC strictly adheres to them and that the control variable influences the MMC plant's response. The presence of the observer improves the prediction of the output, particularly for setpoint changes in the reference signal. We also analyze the prescribed performance, which provides a priori guarantees of closed-loop stability for the proposed controller.

</details>


### [134] [Closed-loop Optimal Fault Detection for Uncertain Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16635)
*Koen Classens,Tjeerd Ickenroth,Jeroen van de Wijdeven,Tom Oomen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 针对连续时间线性时不变不确定系统（开环或闭环），提出一种基于单一Riccati方程求解的鲁棒故障检测滤波器统一设计方法，通过非线性优化和边界Nevanlinna-Pick方法构建最坏情况模型，在光刻机实验级台上验证了故障敏感性与不确定性/扰动抑制的最优折中能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂工程系统中的故障会损害可靠性与安全性，现有方法难以统一处理参数不确定性和动态不确定性，且难以在故障敏感性与建模不确定性/扰动抑制之间取得最优平衡。

Method: 提出一种统一方法，通过非线性优化和边界Nevanlinna-Pick方法构建最坏情况扰动与不确定模型，进而求解单一Riccati方程来设计鲁棒故障检测滤波器，适用于开环或闭环连续时间线性时不变不确定系统。

Result: 基于光刻工业中实验级台的不确定模型进行验证，表明该方法在故障敏感性与建模不确定性/扰动抑制之间实现了最优折中，能够清晰区分残差中的故障与非期望影响，提升故障检测可靠性。

Conclusion: 所提统一方法通过单一Riccati方程和基于最坏情况的模型，有效解决了参数与动态不确定性下的鲁棒故障检测滤波器设计问题，提升了机器的安全性和性能。

Abstract: Faults compromise the reliability and safety of complex engineering systems. The aim of this article is to address the problem of robust fault detection filter design for continuous-time linear time-invariant uncertain systems in open-loop or closed-loop configurations. The developed method offers a unified approach to handle parametric and dynamic uncertainties by solving a single Riccati equation, based on a worst-case disturbance and uncertainty model. This worst-case model is obtained by nonlinear optimization and application of the boundary Nevanlinna-Pick method. The efficacy of the proposed approach is demonstrated using an uncertain model of an experimental reticle stage used in the lithography industry. The results illustrate that an optimal compromise is achieved between sensitivity to faults and rejection of modelling uncertainties and disturbances on the other hand. This capability enables the clear differentiation between faults and undesired effects in residuals, thereby enhancing fault detection reliability, ultimately contributing to improved safety and performance of machines.

</details>


### [135] [Learning practically stabilizing output-feedback nonlinear controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16930)
*Kui Xie,Pablo Krupa,Alberto Bemporad*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种离线学习输出反馈替代控制器的方法，该控制器通过循环神经网络模仿给定的非线性控制器-观测器对，并在训练中结合李雅普诺夫函数学习以保证输入-状态实际稳定性。在连续搅拌釜反应器上验证，结果表明联合学习李雅普诺夫函数优于纯模仿基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决计算昂贵的非线性控制器-观测器对的替代问题，希望在离线学习中不仅实现模仿精度，还能保证输入-状态实际稳定性。

Method: 使用循环动力系统作为替代模型，通过离线闭环输入/输出轨迹模仿给定控制器；在训练目标中融入估计状态轨迹以学习候选李雅普诺夫函数，从而促进输入-状态实际稳定性。

Result: 在非线性连续搅拌釜反应器上验证，采用概率验证方法评估约束满足与实际稳定性；数值结果显示联合学习李雅普诺夫函数的方案优于纯模仿基线。

Conclusion: 离线学习输出反馈替代控制器时，联合学习李雅普诺夫函数能够有效提升系统的实际稳定性，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of learning an output-feedback surrogate controller offline that approximates a given, possibly computationally expensive, nonlinear controller-observer pair. The surrogate is modeled as a recurrent dynamical system and is trained to imitate closed-loop input/output trajectories generated by the given controller. Beyond imitation accuracy, the offline training problem promotes input-to-state practical stability by incorporating estimated state trajectories to learn a candidate Lyapunov function. The approach is validated on a nonlinear continuous stirred tank reactor, where constraint satisfaction and practical stability are assessed through a probabilistic validation approach. The numerical results highlight the benefit of jointly learning the Lyapunov function by comparing against an imitation-only baseline.

</details>


### [136] [Sandbox-Enabled Digital Twin for Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17001)
*Meet Udeshi,Md Raz,Prashanth Krishnamurthy,Ramesh Karri,Farshad Khorrami*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个闭环数字孪生框架，将未修改的控制器二进制运行在SaMOSA Linux沙箱中，其I/O重定向到外部植物模拟器，实现植物条件与控制器行为侧信道的时间同步捕获，为CPS控制器的在线测试、覆盖分析和异常检测提供可观测性基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPS控制器易受故障和恶意攻击影响，包括仅在复杂植物条件下触发的失效；但预部署验证通常依赖植物模型或数字孪生以黑盒方式测试控制器I/O，而运行时侧信道检测虽能补充却处于开环状态、脱离I/O仪器化且由合成输入驱动而非真实植物反馈，因此亟需一种能耦合植物条件与控制器侧信道的闭环验证框架。

Method: 将未修改的控制器二进制部署在SaMOSA Linux沙箱内，将其I/O重定向至外部植物模拟器形成闭环；捕获四种时间同步的控制器侧信道（硬件性能计数器、系统调用、磁盘活动、网络活动）及植物状态；利用编排钩子实现可重复、参数化的运行。

Result: 在OpenPLC运行时上执行Structured Text控制程序，连接Modbus的IEEE 14总线电力系统模型进行演示，并讨论了在机器人系统中的应用；捕获的侧信道成功将控制器行为与模拟植物事件关联。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了CPS控制器在线测试、覆盖分析和异常检测的可观测性基础，弥合了开环侧信道检测与闭环植物-控制器耦合验证之间的差距。

Abstract: Cyber-physical system (CPS) controllers are vulnerable to faults and malicious attacks, including failures triggered only under complex plant conditions, yet pre-deployment validation typically relies on plant models or digital twins that exercise the controller's I/O as a black box. Side-channels, used to detect those run-time behavioral anomalies, are complementary but also open-loop, detached from I/O instrumentation, and driven by synthetic inputs rather than realistic plant feedback. We present a closed-loop digital twin framework that bridges this gap by hosting an unmodified controller binary within the SaMOSA Linux sandbox with its I/O rerouted to an external plant simulator, allowing coupled capture of simulated plant conditions and events alongside the controller's behavioral side-channels. The framework captures four time-synchronized controller side-channels (hardware performance counters, system calls, disk activity, and network activity) alongside plant state, and uses orchestration hooks for repeatable, parameterized runs. We demonstrate the framework on an OpenPLC runtime executing a Structured Text control program against a Modbus-connected IEEE 14-bus power-system model, and also discuss the application to robotics systems. The captured side-channels correlate controller behavior with simulated plant events, establishing an observability foundation for online testing, coverage analysis, and anomaly detection in CPS controllers.

</details>


### [137] [Data-Driven Personalization of Automated Insulin Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17004)
*Ali Kashani,Ali Tavasoli,Heman Shakeri*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对自动胰岛素输送系统的在线数据驱动个性化方法，通过投影梯度下降优化每日血糖风险指标，在FDA认可的UVA/Padova T1D模拟器上验证，经17周适应后时间范围内提高4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AID系统面向人群调参，缺乏在线适应个体间及个体内因饮食、运动、胰岛素敏感性波动导致的胰岛素需求变化的能力。

Method: 将适应问题建模为基于每日血糖风险度量的投影梯度下降，梯度估计设计为衰减噪声和代谢变异性，并利用收缩理论验证优化框架及闭环系统的收敛性。

Result: 在100名成年患者的UVA/Padova T1D模拟器中，在餐时、餐量和胰岛素敏感性变异条件下，4周、8周和17周后TIR分别提升2%、3%和4%。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效实现AID系统的在线个性化自适应，改善血糖风险指标，且具有理论收敛保障。

Abstract: Automated insulin delivery (AID) systems are often tuned for the population and offer limited online adaptation to the inter- and intrapatient variability in insulin needs caused by meal patterns, physical activity, and fluctuations in insulin sensitivity. We present a real-time, data-driven personalization approach that adapts controller parameters using the subject's daily glycemic data. The adaptation is formulated as projected gradient descent on a daily risk metric, where the gradient estimation is designed to attenuate noise and metabolic variability. We use contraction theory to validate the optimization framework and convergence of the closed-loop system under adaptation. In silico experiments on the 100-adult cohort of the FDA-accepted UVA/Padova T1D simulator show that our method improves glycemic risk and increases time-in-range (TIR, 70-180\,mg/dL) by 2%, 3%, and 4% after 4, 8, and 17 weeks, respectively, under variability in meal timing, meal size, and insulin sensitivity.

</details>


### [138] [Optimal Bounded Thrust Powered Descent with Analytical Ground-Collision Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17050)
*Or Nataf,Vitaly Shaferman*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对有界推力动力下降问题，提出了一种基于时间依赖多项式质量近似的分层推力分配方法，实现了解析性的地面碰撞规避制导律，能够在推力饱和情况下保证软着陆性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有有界推力动力下降方法依赖数值优化和轨迹整形约束，缺乏解析性的碰撞规避条件，计算成本高，难以在实时场景中有效兼顾推力饱和与地面碰撞规避。

Method: 采用时间依赖多项式近似质量变化，将有界推力最优控制问题转化为有界线性二次型最优控制问题；通过分层分离推力分配，对水平推力施加硬约束、垂直推力保持无约束，从而解析地推导出地面碰撞规避条件；基于该框架预测饱和与非饱和弧段间的切换时间，在推力持续饱和情况下仍能整形推力加速度剖面以实现软着陆。

Result: 在扰动初始条件网格上的真实仿真中，所提方法实现了精确的无碰撞软着陆性能，验证了将饱和感知制导与地面碰撞规避相结合的重要性。

Conclusion: 该方法具有解析性、计算高效且连续推力剖面便于实时实现，为有界推力动力下降问题提供了一种可解析保证碰撞规避的新途径。

Abstract: The paper proposes a new approach to address the bounded-thrust powered-descent problem while ensuring ground-collision avoidance. A time-dependent polynomial approximation of the mass is employed to formulate a bounded linear-quadratic optimal-control problem that minimizes the thrust-acceleration control effort, terminal miss, and terminal velocity error. The resulting approximation is used to impose a hard constraint on the horizontal thrust profile while keeping the vertical thrust profile unconstrained. The key idea is a hierarchical separation of the thrust allocation, which enables analytical ground-collision avoidance under bounded thrust. Unlike existing bounded-thrust powered-descent approaches based on numerical optimization and trajectory-shaping constraints, the proposed method provides explicit analytical collision-avoidance conditions. Building on this formulation, the guidance law predicts the switching times between saturated and unsaturated arcs and shapes the thrust-acceleration profile to achieve a soft landing, even when the controller remains saturated over extended portions of the trajectory. Owing to its analytical nature, the guidance law is computationally efficient, and its continuous thrust profile facilitates real-time implementation. The proposed method was evaluated over a grid of perturbed initial conditions in realistic simulations, demonstrating accurate collision-free soft-landing performance. The results highlight the importance of combining saturation-aware guidance with ground-collision avoidance under bounded thrust.

</details>


### [139] [Impedance MPC with Patient-Torque Estimation for Knee Rehabilitation Exoskeletons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13485)
*Yongyan Cao,Jinshan Tang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于膝关节康复外骨骼的阻抗模型预测控制框架，通过代数前馈将膝关节动力学简化为常系数标量双积分器，结合卡尔曼扰动状态估计和离线预计算的QP成本逆矩阵，实现了500 Hz运行频率、无偏移跟踪（稳态0.1 mrad）和对痉挛/患者主动力的安全顺应性，优于所有经典阻抗控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 膝关节康复外骨骼需要在强制执行预设关节轨迹的同时，安全地顺应非自主痉挛和患者主动力——这对于任何固定增益阻抗控制器而言是相互矛盾的目标。现有方法难以同时实现精确轨迹跟踪和对患者交互力的柔性顺应。

Method: 提出Impedance Model Predictive Control框架：1) 代数前馈将膝关节动力学简化为常系数标量双积分器；2) 滚动时域二次规划(QP)在硬约束（ISO 13482运动范围、扭矩、速度限制）下计算校正扭矩；3) 基于串联弹性执行器(SEA)弹簧偏转测量的卡尔曼扰动状态估计（无EMG/独立力传感器），实现名义无偏移保证和传感器less的按需辅助(Assist-as-Needed)；4) 常系数状态矩阵允许离线预计算QP成本逆矩阵，实现500 Hz多步长运行。

Result: 在七种控制器基准测试中，500 Hz Kalman MPC无偏移：RMS 0.1 mrad、稳态0.1 mrad、15 Nm痉挛下峰值0.2 mrad；经典阻抗同刚度下稳态偏移515 mrad。无估计器时实现经典阻抗（RMS 4.8 mrad，稳态8.3 mrad）。所有MPC变体满足87 mrad临床标准，经典控制器均不满足。估计器在几个采样周期内近瞬时收敛。

Conclusion: 所提架构在康复外骨骼中实现了精确轨迹跟踪与安全柔顺性的统一，通过直接SEA扭矩传感实现无偏移估计和按需辅助，且可扩展至20自由度MyoSuite myoLeg平台的耦合感知逐关节QP控制。

Abstract: Knee rehabilitation exoskeletons must enforce a prescribed joint trajectory while remaining safely compliant with involuntary spasm and voluntary patient effort-objectives in tension for any fixed-gain impedance controller. We present an Impedance Model Predictive Control framework for knee rehabilitation exoskeletons, demonstrated on a series-elastic-actuator (SEA) platform: an algebraic feedforward reduces the knee dynamics to a constant-coefficient scalar double integrator, and a receding-horizon quadratic program (QP) computes corrective torques while enforcing hard range-of-motion, torque, and velocity limits (ISO 13482). A Kalman disturbance state driven by direct SEA-based torque sensing (the series-elastic spring deflection measured through the elastic element - an intrinsic, EMG-free patient-torque estimate, not a separate load cell) gives a nominal offset-free guarantee and, via its sign and the desired-motion direction, sensorless Assist-as-Needed. The constant state matrix permits offline precomputation of the QP cost inverse, enabling 500 Hz operation with a multi-step horizon. Across seven-controller benchmarks (sinusoidal tracking, isometric hold), the 500 Hz Kalman MPC is offset free 0.1 mrad RMS, 0.1 mrad steady-state, 0.2 mrad peak under 15 Nm spasm, versus a 515 mrad steady-state offset for classical impedance at the same stiffness - the direct-measurement channel converging the estimate near-immediately (within a few sampling periods). Without the estimator it realizes a classical impedance (4.8 mrad RMS, 8.3 mrad steady-state). All MPC variants meet the 87 mrad clinical criterion; no classical controller does. The architecture is formulated for the 20 DOF MyoSuite myoLeg via coupling-aware per-joint QPs.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [140] [The Regional Boundary Reconstruction Problem of the Initial State for Fractional Semilinear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14705)
*Khalid Zguaid,Fatima Zahrae El Alaoui,Delfim F. M. Torres*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了半线性时间分数阶系统的区域边界初始状态重构问题，通过解析方法建立了内部子区域与边界子区域之间的联系，证明了收敛序列并给出了数值仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观测性理论关注整个域或边界上的初始状态重构，但实际中往往只需要恢复特定边界子区域上的初始状态值，本文旨在解决这一区域边界重构问题。

Method: 采用解析方法，假设系统动态生成解析半群；首先通过建立内部子区域来桥接区域观测性与区域边界观测性概念；然后对解析半群和系统非线性施加适当假设，推导出收敛到未知初始状态的序列。

Result: 给出了该问题的主要定理，证明了从定理中推导出的序列能够收敛到期望边界子区域上的未知初始状态；数值模拟结果与理论发现高度一致。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了半线性时间分数阶系统在边界子区域上的初始状态重构理论框架，并通过数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Observability is a fundamental concept in control theory. Its primary purpose is to look into whether it is possible to reconstruct the system's initial state using only the information from its outputs. This paper focuses on the regional reconstruction problem of the initial state for a semilinear time-fractional system, which refers to the possibility of recovering the value of the initial state on a desired boundary sub-region instead of the whole evolution domain or its boundary. To achieve this objective, we use the analytical method, where we suppose that the system's dynamic generates an analytic semigroup. First, by establishing an internal sub-region, we establish a connection between the concepts of regional observability and regional boundary observability; we will later explain how to define the internal sub-region. Then, by imposing suitable assumptions on the analytic semigroup and the system's non-linearity, we give the main theorems of this research from which we deduce a sequence that converges to the unknown initial state on the desired boundary sub-region. Moreover, we present an algorithm that produces some numerical simulations which align closely with our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [141] [Risk or Replace: Efficient Asymptotics for Data-Driven Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14706)
*Poulad Moradi,Joachim Arts,Melvin Drent,Collin Drent*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对一类参数未知的异质性退化系统，提出了一种基于数据驱动的视情维护策略（EOP），通过估计完全信息下最优策略来近似求解高维POMDP，并在一定缩放条件下证明了其遗憾值收敛于零，实验验证了极低的性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际中退化组件的个体参数不可观测，导致最优CBM策略需要求解高维POMDP，计算上不可行。本文旨在开发一种在计算上可行的、数据驱动的CBM策略，使其在大规模实际场景中接近完全信息下的最优策略。

Method: 将问题建模为高维POMDP；提出Estimated Oracle's Optimal Policy (EOP)，利用退化数据估计组件特定参数，然后将估计参数代入Oracle最优策略中执行；引入缩放机制（失效阈值和成本参数同比例增长）来刻画组件寿命和维护成本远大于决策间隔的实际场景；在参数估计量一致的前提下，证明EOP的遗憾值在该缩放机制下收敛于零。

Result: 在真实数据和模拟数据的大量实验中，EOP实现了极低的遗憾值；当最优POMDP策略可精确计算时，EOP的最优性间隙可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 本文提出的EOP策略在异质性退化系统的CBM问题中，能够以可忽略的性能损失接近完全信息Oracle的最优策略，为实际中高维POMDP的CBM决策提供了可行且高效的数据驱动方案。

Abstract: Condition-based maintenance (CBM) is an approach that plans interventions for deteriorating systems according to their observed operational state. CBM reduces unplanned downtime and extends usable lifetime. We study a heterogeneous population of components that degrade over time according to a stochastic processes with non-negative and i.i.d. increments that are characterized by component-specific parameters that remain unobservable to the decision maker. We rely on degradation data to estimate these parameters and determine replacement actions at equidistant epochs. The goal is to minimize the long-run average cost, which incorporates fixed replacement costs, failure costs, and operating costs. This problem can be formulated as a high-dimensional partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP), which is generally intractable. We develop a tractable, data-driven CBM policy that estimates the optimal policy of a hypothetical Oracle that has full information of the underlying degradation parameters and call this policy the Estimated Oracle's Optimal Policy (EOP). We introduce a scaling regime where both the failure thresholds and cost parameters increase proportionally, reflecting practical settings in which component lifetimes and maintenance costs are large relative to the time between two consecutive CBM decision moments. We show that the regret of the EOP, defined as the difference between its long-run average cost and that of the Oracle, converges to zero in the scaling regime when the parameter estimator is consistent. Across extensive experiments using both real and simulated data, the EOP achieves very low regret and, whenever the optimal POMDP policy can be computed exactly, a negligible optimality gap.

</details>


### [142] [PH-KAN: Port-Hamiltonian Kolmogorov-Arnold Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14708)
*Achraf El Messaoudi,Karim Cherifi,Yann Le Gorrec,Yongxin Wu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN）的结构保持非线性端口-哈密顿系统辨识框架（PH-KAN），通过专用KAN块参数化互连矩阵、耗散矩阵、哈密顿量和输入映射，在保持端口-哈密顿约束的同时实现了比标准MLP参数化更可解释的构件表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的机器学习方法在非线性系统辨识中日益流行，但标准模型往往无法保持底层物理结构且难以解释。端口-哈密顿模型虽能提供自然的物理信息表示，但用标准多层感知机参数化时，学习到的构成组件仍难以解释。

Method: 提出PH-KAN模型，使用KAN块分别参数化互连矩阵、耗散矩阵、哈密顿量和输入映射，并通过构造方式强制满足端口-哈密顿约束，使得辨识出的pH组件中的非线性函数可以被显式检查。

Result: 与基于MLP的标准参数化相比，PH-KAN模型在保持端口-哈密顿结构的同时，提供了更可解释的模型。

Conclusion: PH-KAN框架为非线性端口-哈密顿系统的结构保持辨识提供了一种兼具物理一致性和可解释性的有效方案。

Abstract: Data-driven machine learning approaches have become increasingly attractive for nonlinear system identification, but standard models often fail to preserve the underlying physical structure and remain difficult to interpret, especially when no analytical model is available. In this context, port-Hamiltonian (pH) models provide a natural physics-informed representation. However, when these models are parameterized with standard multilayer perceptrons (MLPs), the learned constitutive components often remain poorly interpretable. In this paper, we propose a structure-preserving identification framework for nonlinear port-Hamiltonian systems based on Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs). The proposed PH-KAN model parameterizes the interconnection matrix, dissipation matrix, Hamiltonian, and input mapping using dedicated KAN blocks, while enforcing the port-Hamiltonian constraints by construction. This yields constitutive representations in which the nonlinear functions defining the identified pH components can be explicitly inspected, leading to a more interpretable model than with standard MLP-based parameterizations.

</details>


### [143] [A Discrete KKT Variational Characterization of the Local Minimality of the Mahler Volume in Centrally Symmetric Polytopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14709)
*Daniel Martín Jiménez Cuevas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过离散参数化方法研究中心对称多面体的Mahler泛函，利用径向浸入的体积一阶变分导出KKT驻点条件，证明Hanner轨道在径向伸缩模GL(n,R)的商空间中为严格局部极小点，并通过Hessian矩阵对角占优性验证正定性，结合Hausdorff拓扑下的单纯截断分析建立二次定量稳定性界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服传统光滑连续分析在处理多面体Mahler泛函极小性时出现的退化问题，为Hanner多面体的严格局部极小性提供显式代数解析证明及其在子空间中的拓扑孤立性刻画。

Method: 离散参数化表征Mahler泛函；基于顶点径向浸入导出体积一阶变分并建立KKT条件；利用对角占优性分析径向Hessian矩阵的谱性质；在Hausdorff拓扑下进行单纯截断分析以建立定量稳定性界。

Result: 证明了Hanner轨道在所有维度n≥2下是严格局部极小点；径向Hessian矩阵严格正定；建立了针对局部多面体扰动的二次定量稳定性界。

Conclusion: 该离散框架有效规避了传统连续分析的退化问题，为Hanner构型在中心对称多面体空间中的严格局部极小性与拓扑孤立性提供了显式代数证明。

Abstract: We present a discrete parametric characterization of the Mahler functional V_M for centrally symmetric polytopes in R^n. By formulating the first variation of the volume with respect to the radial immersions of the vertices, we derive an exact KKT stationarity condition. Through this formalism, we demonstrate that the Hanner orbit constitutes a strict local minimum in the stratum of radial dilations modulo GL(n, R), for all dimensions n >= 2. Spectral analysis of the second variation reveals that the radial Hessian matrix is strictly positive definite by diagonal dominance. Coupling this isochoric result with a simplicial truncation analysis in the Hausdorff topology, we establish a quadratic quantitative stability bound against local polyhedral perturbations. This discrete framework eludes the degenerations of traditional smooth continuous analysis, providing an explicit algebraic resolution for the strict local minimality of this geometric configuration and its topological isolation in the subspace of polytopes.

</details>


### [144] [Collective Optimization on Riemannian Manifolds with Bounded Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14884)
*Hui Huang,Dohyun Kim,Hansol Park*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于黎曼流形（具有有界截面曲率）的内蕴共识优化（CBO）框架，直接利用流形的内蕴结构（对数映射和指数映射）建模，而非依赖外部欧几里得嵌入。证明了粒子系统及其McKean-Vlasov动力学的全局适定性，以及均场方程在适当条件下全局收敛到目标函数的最小值。在球面、双曲空间和特殊正交群上的数值实验验证了该方法在流形非凸优化中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共识优化方法多依赖欧几里得空间或外部嵌入处理流形问题，缺乏直接基于黎曼内蕴结构的理论框架，难以保证在流形上的几何一致性和收敛性。

Method: 利用黎曼流形上的对数映射和指数映射（基于内蕴测地距离）直接构建粒子系统，推导其McKean-Vlasov均场极限，并在有界截面曲率条件下建立全局适定性和收敛性分析。

Result: 建立了内蕴CBO粒子系统的全局适定性及均场方程向全局最小值的收敛性理论，在球面、双曲空间和特殊正交群上的数值实验均表现良好。

Conclusion: 内蕴CBO框架为流形上的非凸优化提供了一种几何一致且理论完备的求解途径，避免了外在嵌入的几何失真问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop an intrinsic consensus-based optimization framework on Riemannian manifolds with bounded sectional curvature. In contrast to extrinsic approaches based on an ambient Euclidean embedding, our model is formulated directly in terms of the Riemannian structure, using logarithmic and exponential maps induced by the intrinsic geodesic distance. We prove the global well-posedness of the proposed particle system and its associated McKean--Vlasov dynamics. We also establish the global convergence of the mean-field equation toward a global minimizer of the objective function under suitable conditions. Numerical experiments on the sphere, hyperbolic space, and the special orthogonal group demonstrate the effectiveness of the intrinsic CBO dynamics for nonconvex optimization problems on manifolds.

</details>


### [145] [CVXPY 1.9: Recent Advances in Optimization Modeling Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14891)
*William Zhang,Parth Nobel,Aryaman Jeendgar,Riley Murray,Philipp Schiele,Steven Diamond*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: CVXPY 1.1-1.9版本在凸优化领域语言中引入了统一CQP标准形式、堆叠切片后端加速参数化程序、N维表达式原生支持、变量显式稀疏性、多变量属性、量子信息论相关锥/原子以及规整非线性规划(DNLP)等重大改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动CVXPY语言的发展，通过引入统一化标准形式、性能优化、表达能力扩展以及新问题类型的支持，使凸优化建模更方便、高效且适用范围更广。

Method: 设计并实现了统一CQP标准形式用于规范化；采用堆叠切片后端加速参数化程序；支持N维表达式、显式稀疏变量、多变量属性；引入量子信息论相关的锥和原子；提出DNLP扩展问题处理范畴。

Result: CVXPY 1.1至1.9版本在表达能力、求解效率、问题类型覆盖和编程体验方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过上述设计改进，CVXPY持续演进为一个更强大、灵活且高效的凸优化建模工具。

Abstract: CVXPY is a Python-embedded domain-specific language for convex optimization that lets users express problems in mathematical notation while the system verifies convexity and reduces valid programs to solver-ready form. This paper reports on the major advances from versions 1.1 through 1.9. These include a unified conic quadratic program (CQP) standard form for canonicalization; a stacked-slices backend that accelerates parameterized programs; first-class support for N-dimensional expressions; explicit sparsity for variables; support for multiple variable attributes; cones/atoms relevant to quantum information theory; and the introduction of disciplined nonlinear programming (DNLP). We outline the design, algorithms, and modeling consequences of these features.

</details>


### [146] [Controller and Control Architecture Co-Design via Mixed-Integer System-Level Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14966)
*Chenchen Zhou,Jose Matias*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向动态输出反馈系统的控制器与控制架构协同设计方法。通过有限时域输出反馈系统级综合（OF-SLS）响应优化，将传感器、执行器、链路及时延的离散选择编码为混合整数凸规划问题，并能在相同架构成本下，将非二次不变（QI）架构的性能损失降至最佳QI架构的1/3.8，优于正则化和经典信息流基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理控制器与控制架构联合设计时面临混合整数非凸的困难，常用的替代方案（如固定架构、正则化或限制为QI信息模式）往往过于保守或无法达到最优性能。本文旨在寻找一种既能处理离散架构选择又能保持优化可解性的方法。

Method: 将二进制变量引入有限时域输出反馈系统级综合（OF-SLS）响应优化中，用二进制变量选择传感器、执行器、链路和时延，并利用指示约束在选定延迟之前将不可用的FIR响应块置零。对于实现局部化的OF-SLS架构，该方法等价于一个混合整数凸规划问题，可在给定的有限延迟菜单、FIR时域、可容许架构集和标量化权重下全局求解。

Result: 在车辆队列基准测试中，8748种架构中只有99种与QI兼容。在相同架构成本下，所选非QI OF-SLS架构的性能损失仅为最佳QI架构的1/3.8，并优于正则化方法和经典信息流基线。

Conclusion: 本文提出的OF-SLS协同设计方法能够有效处理非QI架构的优化问题，在性能上显著优于传统的QI限制、正则化及经典信息流方法，为控制体系结构设计提供了更灵活的求解框架。

Abstract: We study controller and control-architecture co-design for dynamic output-feedback systems. The architecture selects active sensors and actuators, sensor-to-actuator links, and link delays, with costs for hardware activation and communication latency. Direct optimization over controller transfer matrices and discrete links is mixed-integer nonconvex; common alternatives fix the architecture, use regularization, or restrict the controller information pattern to a quadratically invariant (QI) class. We instead optimize finite-horizon output-feedback system-level synthesis (OF-SLS) responses. Binary variables select sensors, actuators, links, and delays, and indicator constraints zero unavailable FIR response blocks before the selected delays. For implementation-local OF-SLS architectures, this gives an exact mixed-integer convex program over a prescribed finite delay menu. A global solve certifies the best architecture-response pair for the chosen delay menu, FIR horizon, admissible architecture set, and scalarization weight. The same encoding gives a QI controller-support reference problem. In a vehicle-platoon benchmark, 99 of 8748 architectures are QI-compatible. At equal architecture cost, the selected non-QI OF-SLS architecture reduces performance loss by a factor of 3.8 relative to the best QI architecture and outperforms regularization-based and canonical information-flow baselines.

</details>


### [147] [Real-time nonlinear model predictive control framework for event-triggered switching in industrial batch polymerization process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14976)
*Chenchen Zhou,Zuzhen Ji,Jose Matias*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对间歇聚合反应过程的非线性、无稳态工作点及多相态切换等控制难题，系统地将四种实时NMPC技术（平滑模式混合、高级步进热启动、变量缩放、迭代预算限制）有机结合，实现了无启发式切换逻辑的实时可行控制。通过闭环仿真验证，该方法在约束满足和批次时长方面优于PID和传统NMPC基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 间歇聚合反应控制面临三大挑战：缺乏稳态工作点导致标准线性化方法失效；动力学本质非线性；多相操作引发状态触发的切换行为。现有方法依赖启发式切换逻辑，缺乏系统性理论指导。

Method: 系统性地将四种成熟的实时NMPC技术进行组合：平滑模式混合（smooth mode blending）、高级步进热启动（advanced-step warm starts）、变量缩放（variable scaling）和迭代预算限制（capped iteration budget）。提供了平滑增益选择和切换面定位的实践指导，并明确了平滑引入的近似误差，确保经适当调优后平滑与原切换逻辑在求解器容差水平上数值不可区分。

Result: 在包含估计器回路的工业气-液聚合基准模型上进行了闭环仿真，与PID和传统NMPC基线对比。结果显示：在有限计算资源下改进了约束满足度并缩短了批次持续时间；消融实验量化了各组件单独贡献。

Conclusion: 所提出的系统化组合策略有效解决了间歇聚合反应控制的实时可行性问题，消除了对启发式切换逻辑的依赖，为工业应用提供了可操作的调优指导。
